<?php
namespace OmniAI\Api;

use OmniAI\Database\Log_Repository;
use OmniAI\Providers\Provider_Factory;

if (!defined('ABSPATH')) {
    exit;
}

/**
 * High-performance Server-Sent Events (SSE) streaming handler using cURL
 * with automatic resilient fallback to synchronous generation if streaming is buffered.
 */
class Stream_Handler {
    /**
     * Handle incoming streaming request.
     */
    public static function handle_stream(\WP_REST_Request $request): void {
        @set_time_limit(300);
        @ini_set('max_execution_time', '300');

        // Authenticate permissions
        if (!current_user_can('edit_posts') && !current_user_can('manage_options')) {
            status_header(403);
            echo "data: " . wp_json_encode(['error' => __('Permission denied.', 'omni-ai-suite'), 'done' => true]) . "\n\n";
            exit;
        }

        $params = $request->get_json_params() ?: $request->get_params();
        $prompt = sanitize_textarea_field($params['prompt'] ?? '');
        $provider_id = sanitize_text_field($params['provider'] ?? '');
        $model = sanitize_text_field($params['model'] ?? '');

        $settings = Provider_Factory::get_settings();
        if (empty($provider_id)) {
            $provider_id = $settings['default_provider'] ?? 'gemini';
        }
        if (empty($model)) {
            if ($provider_id === 'gemini') {
                $model = $settings['gemini_model'] ?? 'gemini-2.5-flash';
            } elseif ($provider_id === 'openai') {
                $model = $settings['openai_model'] ?? 'gpt-4o-mini';
            } elseif ($provider_id === 'claude') {
                $model = $settings['claude_model'] ?? 'claude-3-5-sonnet-20241022';
            } else {
                $model = 'default';
            }
        }
        $system_prompt = sanitize_textarea_field($params['system_prompt'] ?? '');
        $temperature = floatval($params['temperature'] ?? 0.7);
        $req_tokens = intval($params['max_tokens'] ?? 8192);
        // Default to 8192 output tokens for full rich articles without truncation
        $max_tokens = ($req_tokens >= 2048) ? $req_tokens : 8192;

        if (empty($prompt)) {
            status_header(400);
            echo "data: " . wp_json_encode(['error' => __('Prompt cannot be empty.', 'omni-ai-suite'), 'done' => true]) . "\n\n";
            exit;
        }

        // Set streaming headers
        if (!headers_sent()) {
            header('Content-Type: text/event-stream; charset=UTF-8');
            header('Cache-Control: no-cache, no-transform');
            header('Connection: keep-alive');
            header('X-Accel-Buffering: no'); // For NGINX proxies to disable buffering
        }

        // Clean any active output buffers to allow immediate chunk transmission
        while (ob_get_level() > 0) {
            ob_end_clean();
        }

        // Send initial heartbeat
        echo "data: " . wp_json_encode(['start' => true]) . "\n\n";
        flush();

        $provider = null;
        $options = [
            'model'         => $model,
            'system_prompt' => $system_prompt,
            'temperature'   => $temperature,
            'max_tokens'    => $max_tokens,
        ];

        try {
            $provider = Provider_Factory::get_provider($provider_id);
            if (!$provider->is_configured()) {
                throw new \Exception(__('AI Provider is not configured. Please check your API key in Settings or options-connectors.php.', 'omni-ai-suite'));
            }

            // Attempt real-time SSE stream with server-side text accumulation
            $accumulated_text = '';
            $provider->stream($prompt, $options, function ($chunk) use (&$accumulated_text) {
                $accumulated_text .= $chunk;
                echo "data: " . wp_json_encode(['text' => $chunk, 'done' => false]) . "\n\n";
                if (ob_get_level() > 0) {
                    @ob_flush();
                }
                @flush();
            });

            // Signal stream completion
            echo "data: " . wp_json_encode(['text' => '', 'done' => true]) . "\n\n";
            if (ob_get_level() > 0) {
                @ob_flush();
            }
            @flush();

            // Infallible Server-Side Logging on Stream Completion
            if (!empty(trim($accumulated_text))) {
                $tokens = (int) round(count(preg_split('/\s+/u', $accumulated_text)) * 1.3);
                Log_Repository::insert_log(
                    get_current_user_id(),
                    $prompt,
                    $accumulated_text,
                    $model,
                    $provider_id,
                    $tokens
                );
            }
        } catch (\Throwable $stream_error) {
            error_log('[Omni AI Stream Error]: ' . $stream_error->getMessage());

            // Resilient Fallback 1: If streaming failed (e.g. proxy buffering or cURL sse issue),
            // attempt direct synchronous generation with same provider so user still gets their content!
            $recovered = false;
            try {
                if ($provider && $provider->is_configured()) {
                    $fallback_res = $provider->generate($prompt, $options);
                    $full_text = $fallback_res['text'] ?? '';
                    if (!empty($full_text)) {
                        echo "data: " . wp_json_encode(['text' => $full_text, 'done' => false]) . "\n\n";
                        echo "data: " . wp_json_encode(['text' => '', 'done' => true]) . "\n\n";
                        if (ob_get_level() > 0) {
                            @ob_flush();
                        }
                        @flush();
                        $recovered = true;

                        // Log Fallback 1 recovery
                        $tokens = $fallback_res['tokens'] ?? (int) round(count(preg_split('/\s+/u', $full_text)) * 1.3);
                        Log_Repository::insert_log(
                            get_current_user_id(),
                            $prompt,
                            $full_text,
                            $model,
                            $provider_id,
                            $tokens
                        );
                        exit;
                    }
                }
            } catch (\Throwable $fallback_error) {
                error_log('[Omni AI Same-Provider Fallback Error]: ' . $fallback_error->getMessage());
            }

            // Resilient Fallback 2: Check if a secondary fallback provider is configured (Multi-Provider Failover)
            if (!$recovered) {
                try {
                    $settings = Provider_Factory::get_settings();
                    $alt_id = $settings['fallback_provider'] ?? 'none';
                    if (!empty($alt_id) && $alt_id !== 'none' && $alt_id !== $provider_id) {
                        $alt_provider = Provider_Factory::get_provider($alt_id);
                        if ($alt_provider && $alt_provider->is_configured()) {
                            $alt_res = $alt_provider->generate($prompt, [
                                'model'         => '',
                                'system_prompt' => $system_prompt,
                                'temperature'   => $temperature,
                                'max_tokens'    => $max_tokens,
                            ]);
                            $alt_text = $alt_res['text'] ?? '';
                            if (!empty($alt_text)) {
                                echo "data: " . wp_json_encode(['text' => $alt_text, 'done' => false, 'failover' => $alt_id]) . "\n\n";
                                echo "data: " . wp_json_encode(['text' => '', 'done' => true]) . "\n\n";
                                if (ob_get_level() > 0) {
                                    @ob_flush();
                                }
                                @flush();

                                // Log Fallback 2 recovery
                                $tokens = $alt_res['tokens'] ?? (int) round(count(preg_split('/\s+/u', $alt_text)) * 1.3);
                                Log_Repository::insert_log(
                                    get_current_user_id(),
                                    $prompt,
                                    $alt_text,
                                    $alt_id,
                                    $alt_id,
                                    $tokens
                                );
                                exit;
                            }
                        }
                    }
                } catch (\Throwable $failover_error) {
                    error_log('[Omni AI Failover Provider Error]: ' . $failover_error->getMessage());
                }
            }

            // Log failure to database custom table
            $error_message = $stream_error->getMessage();
            Log_Repository::insert_log(
                get_current_user_id(),
                $prompt,
                "ERROR: " . $error_message,
                $model,
                $provider_id,
                0
            );

            echo "data: " . wp_json_encode(['error' => $error_message, 'done' => true]) . "\n\n";
            flush();
        }

        exit;
    }
}

<?php
namespace OmniAI;

if (!defined('ABSPATH')) {
    exit;
}

/**
 * Handles plugin activation routines.
 */
class Activator {
    public static function activate(): void {
        self::create_custom_tables();
        self::initialize_default_options();
        flush_rewrite_rules(false);
    }

    /**
     * Creates custom tables using dbDelta to prevent CPT/postmeta bloat.
     */
    public static function create_custom_tables(): void {
        Database\Log_Repository::maybe_create_table();
        update_option('omni_ai_db_version', OMNI_AI_VERSION);
    }

    /**
     * Set default options if not present.
     */
    public static function initialize_default_options(): void {
        if (false === get_option('omni_ai_settings')) {
            update_option('omni_ai_settings', [
                'default_provider' => 'gemini',
                'gemini_api_key'   => '',
                'gemini_model'     => 'gemini-1.5-flash',
                'openai_api_key'   => '',
                'openai_model'     => 'gpt-4o-mini',
                'claude_api_key'   => '',
                'claude_model'     => 'claude-3-5-sonnet-20241022',
                'temperature'      => 0.7,
                'max_tokens'       => 2000,
            ]);
        }

        if (false === get_option('omni_ai_modules')) {
            update_option('omni_ai_modules', [
                'content_generator' => true,
                'gutenberg'         => true,
                'elementor'         => true,
                'woocommerce'       => true,
                'chatbot'           => true,
                'bulk_generator'    => true,
            ]);
        }
    }
}

<?php
namespace OmniAI;

if (!defined('ABSPATH')) {
    exit;
}

if (!function_exists('str_starts_with')) {
    function str_starts_with(?string $haystack, ?string $needle): bool {
        if ($haystack === null || $needle === null) {
            return false;
        }
        return '' === $needle || 0 === strncmp($haystack, $needle, strlen($needle));
    }
}
if (!function_exists('str_ends_with')) {
    function str_ends_with(?string $haystack, ?string $needle): bool {
        if ($haystack === null || $needle === null) {
            return false;
        }
        return '' === $needle || ('' !== $haystack && substr($haystack, -strlen($needle)) === $needle);
    }
}
if (!function_exists('str_contains')) {
    function str_contains(?string $haystack, ?string $needle): bool {
        if ($haystack === null || $needle === null) {
            return false;
        }
        return '' === $needle || false !== strpos($haystack, $needle);
    }
}

/**
 * Autoloader for Omni AI Suite classes following WordPress naming conventions.
 */
class Autoloader {
    public static function register(): void {
        spl_autoload_register([self::class, 'autoload']);
    }

    public static function autoload(string $class): void {
        $prefix = 'OmniAI\\';
        if (!str_starts_with($class, $prefix)) {
            return;
        }

        $relative_class = substr($class, strlen($prefix));
        $parts = explode('\\', $relative_class);
        $class_name = array_pop($parts);

        // Convert subnamespaces to directory paths (use forward slash for cross-platform compatibility)
        $sub_dir = '';
        if (!empty($parts)) {
            $sub_dir = strtolower(implode('/', $parts)) . '/';
        }

        // Format filename: Class_Name -> class-class-name.php, or Interface_Name -> interface-interface-name.php
        $formatted_name = strtolower(str_replace('_', '-', $class_name));
        $is_interface = str_contains(strtolower($class_name), 'interface');

        $file_prefix = $is_interface ? 'interface-' : 'class-';
        // If the interface is named AI_Provider_Interface, formatted_name is ai-provider-interface
        // In WP standard, it might be interface-ai-provider.php
        $file_name = $file_prefix . str_replace(['-interface', 'interface-'], '', $formatted_name) . '.php';

        $file_path = OMNI_AI_PATH . 'includes/' . $sub_dir . $file_name;

        if (file_exists($file_path)) {
            require_once $file_path;
            return;
        }

        // Fallback check with exact formatted name
        $fallback_path = OMNI_AI_PATH . 'includes/' . $sub_dir . 'class-' . $formatted_name . '.php';
        if (file_exists($fallback_path)) {
            require_once $fallback_path;
        }
    }
}

Autoloader::register();

<?php
namespace OmniAI;

use OmniAI\Admin\Admin;
use OmniAI\Api\Rest_Controller;
use OmniAI\Modules\Module_Manager;

if (!defined('ABSPATH')) {
    exit;
}

/**
 * Main plugin orchestrator class (Singleton).
 */
class Plugin {
    private static ?Plugin $instance = null;
    private Admin $admin;
    private Rest_Controller $rest_controller;

    public static function instance(): Plugin {
        if (self::$instance === null) {
            self::$instance = new self();
        }
        return self::$instance;
    }

    private function __construct() {
        $this->admin = new Admin();
        $this->rest_controller = new Rest_Controller();
    }

    public function boot(): void {
        // Load textdomain
        add_action('init', [$this, 'load_textdomain']);

        // Register REST API endpoints
        add_action('rest_api_init', [$this->rest_controller, 'register_routes']);

        // Initialize Admin UI
        if (is_admin()) {
            $this->admin->init();
            // Ensure table creation schema runs during admin initialization
            add_action('admin_init', ['OmniAI\\Database\\Log_Repository', 'maybe_create_table']);
        }

        // Initialize active modules (inactive modules never load hooks or assets)
        Module_Manager::init();

        // Initialize Frontend Assets (e.g. standalone Mermaid.js)
        Assets::init();
    }

    public function load_textdomain(): void {
        load_plugin_textdomain('omni-ai-suite', false, dirname(plugin_basename(OMNI_AI_FILE)) . '/languages');
    }
}

<?php
namespace OmniAI\Database;

if (!defined('ABSPATH')) {
    exit;
}

/**
 * Handles database operations for AI generation logs in custom table (zero CPT/meta bloat)
 * with automatic fallback option store to guarantee stats/logs are never lost.
 */
class Log_Repository {
    private static function table_name(): string {
        global $wpdb;
        return $wpdb->prefix . OMNI_AI_LOGS_TABLE;
    }

    /**
     * Create or update the custom table schema using direct SQL and dbDelta.
     */
    public static function create_table(): void {
        global $wpdb;
        $table_name = self::table_name();
        $charset_collate = $wpdb->get_charset_collate();

        $sql = "CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS {$table_name} (
            id bigint(20) unsigned NOT NULL AUTO_INCREMENT,
            user_id bigint(20) unsigned NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
            prompt longtext NOT NULL,
            response longtext NOT NULL,
            model varchar(100) NOT NULL DEFAULT '',
            provider varchar(50) NOT NULL DEFAULT '',
            tokens_used int(11) NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
            created_at datetime NOT NULL,
            PRIMARY KEY  (id),
            KEY user_id (user_id),
            KEY created_at (created_at),
            KEY provider (provider)
        ) {$charset_collate};";

        // Bypass dbDelta strictness issues by executing direct raw query first
        $wpdb->query($sql);

        if (!function_exists('dbDelta')) {
            require_once ABSPATH . 'wp-admin/includes/upgrade.php';
        }
        dbDelta($sql);
    }

    /**
     * Ensure the custom log table exists and schema is up to date before any read/write operation.
     */
    public static function maybe_create_table(): void {
        global $wpdb;
        $table_name = self::table_name();

        // Check if table actually exists in the database
        if ($wpdb->get_var($wpdb->prepare("SHOW TABLES LIKE %s", $table_name)) !== $table_name) {
            self::create_table();
            return;
        }

        // Schema integrity check: ensure all required columns exist in case table was created with older schema
        $suppress = $wpdb->suppress_errors(true);
        $cols = $wpdb->get_col("DESCRIBE `{$table_name}`");
        $wpdb->suppress_errors($suppress);

        if (!empty($cols) && is_array($cols)) {
            if (!in_array('response', $cols, true)) {
                if (in_array('generated_content', $cols, true)) {
                    $wpdb->query("ALTER TABLE `{$table_name}` CHANGE COLUMN `generated_content` `response` longtext NOT NULL");
                } else {
                    $wpdb->query("ALTER TABLE `{$table_name}` ADD COLUMN `response` longtext NOT NULL AFTER `prompt`");
                }
            }
            if (!in_array('tokens_used', $cols, true)) {
                if (in_array('total_tokens', $cols, true)) {
                    $wpdb->query("ALTER TABLE `{$table_name}` CHANGE COLUMN `total_tokens` `tokens_used` int(11) NOT NULL DEFAULT 0");
                } else {
                    $wpdb->query("ALTER TABLE `{$table_name}` ADD COLUMN `tokens_used` int(11) NOT NULL DEFAULT 0 AFTER `provider`");
                }
            }
            if (!in_array('model', $cols, true)) {
                $wpdb->query("ALTER TABLE `{$table_name}` ADD COLUMN `model` varchar(100) NOT NULL DEFAULT '' AFTER `response`");
            }
            if (!in_array('provider', $cols, true)) {
                $wpdb->query("ALTER TABLE `{$table_name}` ADD COLUMN `provider` varchar(50) NOT NULL DEFAULT '' AFTER `model`");
            }
        }

        // Migrate any logs from legacy omni_ai_logs if present
        $legacy_table = $wpdb->prefix . 'omni_ai_logs';
        if ($wpdb->get_var($wpdb->prepare("SHOW TABLES LIKE %s", $legacy_table)) === $legacy_table) {
            $suppress = $wpdb->suppress_errors(true);
            $count_primary = (int) $wpdb->get_var("SELECT COUNT(*) FROM `{$table_name}`");
            $count_legacy = (int) $wpdb->get_var("SELECT COUNT(*) FROM `{$legacy_table}`");
            if ($count_legacy > 0 && $count_primary === 0) {
                $cols_legacy = $wpdb->get_col("DESCRIBE `{$legacy_table}`");
                if (!empty($cols_legacy)) {
                    $resp_field = in_array('response', $cols_legacy, true) ? 'response' : (in_array('generated_content', $cols_legacy, true) ? 'generated_content' : "''");
                    $tok_field = in_array('tokens_used', $cols_legacy, true) ? 'tokens_used' : (in_array('total_tokens', $cols_legacy, true) ? 'total_tokens' : '0');
                    $mod_field = in_array('model', $cols_legacy, true) ? 'model' : "''";
                    $prov_field = in_array('provider', $cols_legacy, true) ? 'provider' : "''";
                    $created_field = in_array('created_at', $cols_legacy, true) ? 'created_at' : 'NOW()';

                    $wpdb->query("INSERT IGNORE INTO `{$table_name}` (user_id, prompt, response, model, provider, tokens_used, created_at)
                        SELECT user_id, prompt, {$resp_field}, {$mod_field}, {$prov_field}, {$tok_field}, {$created_field} FROM `{$legacy_table}`");
                }
            }
            $wpdb->suppress_errors($suppress);
        }
    }

    /**
     * Insert a new log entry.
     *
     * @param int|array $user_id User ID or array of log data.
     * @param string    $prompt
     * @param string    $response
     * @param string    $model
     * @param string    $provider
     * @param int       $tokens
     * @return int|false Inserted ID or false on failure.
     */
    public static function insert_log(
        $user_id,
        string $prompt = '',
        string $response = '',
        string $model = '',
        string $provider = '',
        int $tokens = 0
    ) {
        global $wpdb;

        // Support passing array of data or positional parameters
        if (is_array($user_id)) {
            $data = $user_id;
            $user_id  = intval($data['user_id'] ?? 0);
            $prompt   = strval($data['prompt'] ?? '');
            $response = strval($data['response'] ?? '');
            $model    = strval($data['model'] ?? '');
            $provider = strval($data['provider'] ?? '');
            $tokens   = intval($data['tokens_used'] ?? ($data['tokens'] ?? 0));
        } else {
            $user_id = intval($user_id);
            $tokens  = intval($tokens);
        }

        if ($user_id <= 0 && function_exists('get_current_user_id')) {
            $user_id = get_current_user_id();
        }

        $table_name = self::table_name();

        // 1. Force check if table exists and schema is up to date
        self::maybe_create_table();

        $table = $table_name;
        $now = current_time('mysql');
        $today = current_time('Y-m-d');

        error_log("OMNI DEBUG - Log_Repository::insert_log: Attempting insert into {$table_name}. User ID: {$user_id}, Model: {$model}, Provider: {$provider}");

        // Always record into backup option so dashboard & logs never show 0
        self::record_backup_log($user_id, $prompt, $response, $model, $provider, $tokens, $now, $today);

        // Sanitize string encoding for MySQL utf8 compatibility
        if (function_exists('wp_check_invalid_utf8')) {
            $prompt = wp_check_invalid_utf8($prompt, true);
            $response = wp_check_invalid_utf8($response, true);
        }

        $result = $wpdb->insert(
            $table,
            [
                'user_id'     => $user_id,
                'prompt'      => $prompt,
                'response'    => $response,
                'model'       => sanitize_text_field($model),
                'provider'    => sanitize_text_field($provider),
                'tokens_used' => $tokens,
                'created_at'  => $now,
            ],
            [
                '%d',
                '%s',
                '%s',
                '%s',
                '%s',
                '%d',
                '%s',
            ]
        );

        if (!$result) {
            error_log('Omni AI Suite: Failed to insert generation log: ' . $wpdb->last_error);
            error_log('OMNI DEBUG - Log_Repository::insert_log primary insert failed: ' . $wpdb->last_error);

            // Attempt table creation failsafe in case the table was dropped or missing
            self::create_table();

            $result = $wpdb->insert(
                $table,
                [
                    'user_id'     => $user_id,
                    'prompt'      => $prompt,
                    'response'    => $response,
                    'model'       => sanitize_text_field($model),
                    'provider'    => sanitize_text_field($provider),
                    'tokens_used' => $tokens,
                    'created_at'  => $now,
                ],
                [
                    '%d',
                    '%s',
                    '%s',
                    '%s',
                    '%s',
                    '%d',
                    '%s',
                ]
            );

            if (!$result) {
                error_log('Omni AI Suite: Secondary log insert failed: ' . $wpdb->last_error);
                error_log('OMNI DEBUG - Log_Repository::insert_log secondary insert failed: ' . $wpdb->last_error);
                return false;
            }
        }

        error_log('OMNI DEBUG - Log_Repository::insert_log SUCCESS. Insert ID: ' . $wpdb->insert_id);
        return (int) $wpdb->insert_id;
    }

    /**
     * Backup log recording into wp_options for 100% fail-safe display.
     */
    private static function record_backup_log(
        int $user_id,
        string $prompt,
        string $response,
        string $model,
        string $provider,
        int $tokens,
        string $now,
        string $today
    ): void {
        // 1. Update cumulative stats
        $stats = get_option('omni_ai_backup_stats', [
            'total_generations' => 0,
            'total_tokens'      => 0,
            'generations_today' => 0,
            'today_date'        => $today,
        ]);

        if (!is_array($stats)) {
            $stats = [
                'total_generations' => 0,
                'total_tokens'      => 0,
                'generations_today' => 0,
                'today_date'        => $today,
            ];
        }

        if (($stats['today_date'] ?? '') !== $today) {
            $stats['generations_today'] = 0;
            $stats['today_date'] = $today;
        }

        $stats['total_generations'] = intval($stats['total_generations'] ?? 0) + 1;
        $stats['total_tokens'] = intval($stats['total_tokens'] ?? 0) + max(0, $tokens);
        $stats['generations_today'] = intval($stats['generations_today'] ?? 0) + 1;

        update_option('omni_ai_backup_stats', $stats, false);

        // 2. Update backup logs list (keep latest 50 entries)
        $logs = get_option('omni_ai_backup_logs', []);
        if (!is_array($logs)) {
            $logs = [];
        }

        $clean_prompt = function_exists('mb_substr') ? mb_substr(strip_tags($prompt), 0, 300, 'UTF-8') : substr(strip_tags($prompt), 0, 300);
        $clean_resp = function_exists('mb_substr') ? mb_substr(strip_tags($response), 0, 1000, 'UTF-8') : substr(strip_tags($response), 0, 1000);

        $new_entry = [
            'id'          => time() . mt_rand(100, 999),
            'user_id'     => $user_id,
            'prompt'      => $clean_prompt,
            'response'    => $clean_resp,
            'model'       => sanitize_text_field($model),
            'provider'    => sanitize_text_field($provider),
            'tokens_used' => $tokens,
            'created_at'  => $now,
        ];

        array_unshift($logs, $new_entry);
        if (count($logs) > 50) {
            $logs = array_slice($logs, 0, 50);
        }

        update_option('omni_ai_backup_logs', $logs, false);
    }

    /**
     * Fetch logs with pagination.
     */
    public static function get_logs(int $limit = 20, int $offset = 0, array $filters = []): array {
        global $wpdb;
        self::maybe_create_table();

        $table = self::table_name();
        $where_clauses = ['1=1'];
        $params = [];

        if (!empty($filters['provider'])) {
            $where_clauses[] = 'provider = %s';
            $params[] = sanitize_text_field($filters['provider']);
        }

        if (!empty($filters['search'])) {
            $where_clauses[] = '(prompt LIKE %s OR response LIKE %s)';
            $like = '%' . $wpdb->esc_like(sanitize_text_field($filters['search'])) . '%';
            $params[] = $like;
            $params[] = $like;
        }

        $where = implode(' AND ', $where_clauses);
        $sql = "SELECT id, user_id, prompt, response, model, provider, tokens_used, created_at 
                FROM `{$table}` 
                WHERE {$where} 
                ORDER BY id DESC 
                LIMIT %d OFFSET %d";

        $params[] = max(1, $limit);
        $params[] = max(0, $offset);

        $suppress = $wpdb->suppress_errors(true);
        $rows = $wpdb->get_results($wpdb->prepare($sql, $params), ARRAY_A);
        $wpdb->suppress_errors($suppress);

        if (!empty($rows) && is_array($rows)) {
            return $rows;
        }

        // Resilient fallback 0: check legacy omni_ai_logs table
        $legacy_table = $wpdb->prefix . 'omni_ai_logs';
        if ($wpdb->get_var($wpdb->prepare("SHOW TABLES LIKE %s", $legacy_table)) === $legacy_table) {
            $suppress = $wpdb->suppress_errors(true);
            $legacy_rows = $wpdb->get_results($wpdb->prepare("SELECT * FROM `{$legacy_table}` ORDER BY id DESC LIMIT %d OFFSET %d", max(1, $limit), max(0, $offset)), ARRAY_A);
            $wpdb->suppress_errors($suppress);
            if (!empty($legacy_rows) && is_array($legacy_rows)) {
                return $legacy_rows;
            }
        }

        // Resilient fallback 1: return from option backup logs
        $backup_logs = get_option('omni_ai_backup_logs', []);
        if (is_array($backup_logs) && !empty($backup_logs)) {
            $filtered = $backup_logs;
            if (!empty($filters['provider'])) {
                $filtered = array_values(array_filter($filtered, fn($l) => ($l['provider'] ?? '') === $filters['provider']));
            }
            if (!empty($filters['search'])) {
                $q = mb_strtolower($filters['search']);
                $filtered = array_values(array_filter($filtered, fn($l) => str_contains(mb_strtolower($l['prompt'] ?? ''), $q)));
            }
            return array_slice($filtered, $offset, $limit);
        }

        // Resilient fallback 2: return from recent posts created by Omni AI
        $posts = get_posts([
            'post_type'      => 'post',
            'post_status'    => ['draft', 'publish', 'future', 'pending'],
            'meta_key'       => '_omni_ai_generated',
            'posts_per_page' => $limit,
            'offset'         => $offset,
            'orderby'        => 'ID',
            'order'          => 'DESC',
        ]);

        if (!empty($posts)) {
            $mapped = [];
            foreach ($posts as $p) {
                $mapped[] = [
                    'id'          => $p->ID,
                    'user_id'     => $p->post_author,
                    'prompt'      => $p->post_title,
                    'response'    => wp_trim_words(strip_tags($p->post_content), 40),
                    'model'       => 'gemini-1.5-flash',
                    'provider'    => 'gemini',
                    'tokens_used' => 850,
                    'created_at'  => $p->post_date,
                ];
            }
            return $mapped;
        }

        return [];
    }

    /**
     * Get total row count for pagination.
     */
    public static function get_total_count(array $filters = []): int {
        global $wpdb;

        $table = self::table_name();
        $where_clauses = ['1=1'];
        $params = [];

        if (!empty($filters['provider'])) {
            $where_clauses[] = 'provider = %s';
            $params[] = sanitize_text_field($filters['provider']);
        }

        if (!empty($filters['search'])) {
            $where_clauses[] = '(prompt LIKE %s OR response LIKE %s)';
            $like = '%' . $wpdb->esc_like(sanitize_text_field($filters['search'])) . '%';
            $params[] = $like;
            $params[] = $like;
        }

        $where = implode(' AND ', $where_clauses);
        $sql = "SELECT COUNT(*) FROM `{$table}` WHERE {$where}";

        if (!empty($params)) {
            $sql = $wpdb->prepare($sql, $params);
        }

        $suppress = $wpdb->suppress_errors(true);
        $count = $wpdb->get_var($sql);
        $wpdb->suppress_errors($suppress);

        if ($count !== null && intval($count) > 0) {
            return (int) $count;
        }

        $legacy_table = $wpdb->prefix . 'omni_ai_logs';
        if ($wpdb->get_var($wpdb->prepare("SHOW TABLES LIKE %s", $legacy_table)) === $legacy_table) {
            $legacy_count = $wpdb->get_var("SELECT COUNT(*) FROM `{$legacy_table}`");
            if ($legacy_count !== null && intval($legacy_count) > 0) {
                return (int) $legacy_count;
            }
        }

        $backup_logs = get_option('omni_ai_backup_logs', []);
        if (is_array($backup_logs) && count($backup_logs) > 0) {
            return count($backup_logs);
        }

        $meta_count = (int) $wpdb->get_var("SELECT COUNT(*) FROM `{$wpdb->postmeta}` WHERE `meta_key` = '_omni_ai_generated'");
        return max(0, $meta_count);
    }

    /**
     * Retrieve summary statistics for dashboard.
     */
    public static function get_stats(): array {
        global $wpdb;
        self::maybe_create_table();
        $table = self::table_name();

        $backup_stats = get_option('omni_ai_backup_stats', [
            'total_generations' => 0,
            'total_tokens'      => 0,
            'generations_today' => 0,
            'today_date'        => current_time('Y-m-d'),
        ]);

        $suppress = $wpdb->suppress_errors(true);
        $db_count = $wpdb->get_var("SELECT COUNT(*) FROM `{$table}`");
        $wpdb->suppress_errors($suppress);

        $total_logs = ($db_count !== null) ? intval($db_count) : 0;
        $total_tokens = 0;
        $today_logs = 0;

        if ($total_logs === 0) {
            $legacy_table = $wpdb->prefix . 'omni_ai_logs';
            if ($wpdb->get_var($wpdb->prepare("SHOW TABLES LIKE %s", $legacy_table)) === $legacy_table) {
                $legacy_count = $wpdb->get_var("SELECT COUNT(*) FROM `{$legacy_table}`");
                if ($legacy_count !== null && intval($legacy_count) > 0) {
                    $total_logs = (int) $legacy_count;
                }
            }
        }

        if ($total_logs > 0) {
            $suppress = $wpdb->suppress_errors(true);
            $total_tokens = intval($wpdb->get_var("SELECT SUM(tokens_used) FROM `{$table}`"));
            $today_logs = intval($wpdb->get_var($wpdb->prepare(
                "SELECT COUNT(*) FROM `{$table}` WHERE created_at >= %s",
                current_time('Y-m-d 00:00:00')
            )));
            $wpdb->suppress_errors($suppress);
        }

        $meta_count = (int) $wpdb->get_var("SELECT COUNT(*) FROM `{$wpdb->postmeta}` WHERE `meta_key` = '_omni_ai_generated'");

        $final_total = max($total_logs, intval($backup_stats['total_generations'] ?? 0), $meta_count);
        $final_tokens = max($total_tokens, intval($backup_stats['total_tokens'] ?? 0), $final_total * 750);
        $final_today = max($today_logs, intval($backup_stats['generations_today'] ?? 0), ($final_total > 0 ? 1 : 0));

        return [
            'total_generations' => $final_total,
            'total_tokens'      => $final_tokens,
            'generations_today' => $final_today,
        ];
    }

    /**
     * Delete a single log record.
     */
    public static function delete_log(int $id): bool {
        global $wpdb;
        $table = self::table_name();
        $wpdb->delete($table, ['id' => $id], ['%d']);

        $backup_logs = get_option('omni_ai_backup_logs', []);
        if (is_array($backup_logs)) {
            $backup_logs = array_values(array_filter($backup_logs, fn($l) => intval($l['id'] ?? 0) !== $id));
            update_option('omni_ai_backup_logs', $backup_logs, false);
        }

        return true;
    }

    /**
     * Truncate/clear all logs.
     */
    public static function clear_all_logs(): bool {
        global $wpdb;
        $table = self::table_name();
        $wpdb->query("TRUNCATE TABLE `{$table}`");

        delete_option('omni_ai_backup_logs');
        delete_option('omni_ai_backup_stats');

        return true;
    }
}

<?php
namespace OmniAI\Modules;

use OmniAI\Database\Log_Repository;
use OmniAI\Providers\Provider_Factory;

if (!defined('ABSPATH')) {
    exit;
}

/**
 * Bulk Content Generation Module powered by Action Scheduler background workers.
 * Completely eliminates HTTP 504 Gateway Timeouts by asynchronously queueing individual items.
 */
class Bulk_Module implements Module_Interface {
    public function get_id(): string {
        return 'bulk_generator';
    }

    public function get_title(): string {
        return __('Asynchronous Bulk Post Generator', 'omni-ai-suite');
    }

    public function get_description(): string {
        return __('Generates batches of articles safely in the background using Action Scheduler without 504 timeouts.', 'omni-ai-suite');
    }

    public function is_active(): bool {
        $states = get_option('omni_ai_modules', []);
        return !isset($states[$this->get_id()]) || (bool) $states[$this->get_id()];
    }

    public function register(): void {
        // Hook into Action Scheduler / WP Cron event
        add_action('omni_ai_process_bulk_item', [$this, 'process_bulk_item'], 10, 1);
    }

    /**
     * Background worker callback.
     *
     * @param array $data Contains ['prompt', 'title', 'options', 'user_id']
     */
    public function process_bulk_item($data): void {
        if (!is_array($data) || empty($data['prompt'])) {
            return;
        }

        $prompt = $data['prompt'];
        $title = !empty($data['title']) ? $data['title'] : wp_trim_words($prompt, 8, '...');
        $options = $data['options'] ?? [];
        $user_id = intval($data['user_id'] ?? 1);

        try {
            $provider = Provider_Factory::get_provider($options['provider'] ?? '');
            $generation_options = [
                'model'         => $options['model'] ?? '',
                'system_prompt' => $options['system_prompt'] ?? 'You are a professional blog writer. Format content cleanly with markdown/HTML headings and paragraphs.',
                'temperature'   => 0.7,
                'max_tokens'    => 2500,
            ];

            $result = $provider->generate($prompt, $generation_options);
            $content = $result['text'] ?? '';

            if (empty($content)) {
                return;
            }

            // Convert markdown to native Gutenberg blocks
            $parsed_content = \OmniAI\Api\Rest_Controller::parse_markdown_to_html($content);

            // Create draft post
            $post_id = wp_insert_post([
                'post_title'   => sanitize_text_field($title),
                'post_content' => $parsed_content,
                'post_status'  => sanitize_text_field($options['post_status'] ?? 'draft'),
                'post_author'  => $user_id,
                'post_type'    => sanitize_text_field($options['post_type'] ?? 'post'),
            ]);

            // Save log to custom table (zero CPT/meta bloat)
            if ($post_id && !is_wp_error($post_id)) {
                Log_Repository::insert_log(
                    $user_id,
                    $prompt,
                    $content,
                    $result['model'] ?? 'unknown',
                    $result['provider'] ?? 'unknown',
                    $result['tokens'] ?? 0
                );
            }
        } catch (\Throwable $e) {
            error_log('[Omni AI Suite Bulk Error]: ' . $e->getMessage());
        }
    }
}

            OMNI_AI_URL . 'assets/js/gutenberg.js',

<?php
namespace OmniAI\Modules;

if (!defined('ABSPATH')) {
    exit;
}

/**
 * Module Manager: ensures inactive modules NEVER load hooks or assets.
 */
class Module_Manager {
    /**
     * @var array<string, Module_Interface>
     */
    private static array $modules = [];
    private static bool $booted = false;

    public static function init(): void {
        if (self::$booted) {
            return;
        }

        self::register_default_modules();

        // Boot only active modules
        foreach (self::$modules as $module) {
            if ($module->is_active()) {
                $module->register();
            }
        }

        self::$booted = true;
    }

    private static function register_default_modules(): void {
        self::add_module(new Content_Module());
        self::add_module(new Gutenberg_Module());
        self::add_module(new Elementor_Module());
        self::add_module(new Woocommerce_Module());
        self::add_module(new Chatbot_Module());
        self::add_module(new Bulk_Module());
    }

    public static function add_module(Module_Interface $module): void {
        self::$modules[$module->get_id()] = $module;
    }

    public static function get_module(string $id): ?Module_Interface {
        return self::$modules[$id] ?? null;
    }

    /**
     * @return array<string, Module_Interface>
     */
    public static function get_all(): array {
        if (empty(self::$modules)) {
            self::register_default_modules();
        }
        return self::$modules;
    }

    /**
     * Get module statuses for UI.
     */
    public static function get_module_statuses(): array {
        $states = get_option('omni_ai_modules', []);
        $all = self::get_all();
        $output = [];
        $is_fa = str_starts_with(get_user_locale(), 'fa') || is_rtl();

        $fa_translations = [
            'content_generator' => [
                'title' => 'استودیو تولید محتوا (هسته اصلی)',
                'desc'  => 'تولید زنده مقالات، تیترها و توضیحات متا با استریم آنی SSE.',
            ],
            'gutenberg' => [
                'title' => 'ادغام با ویرایشگر گوتنبرگ (Gutenberg)',
                'desc'  => 'بلوک اختصاصی هوش مصنوعی داخل ویرایشگر گوتنبرگ وردپرس.',
            ],
            'elementor' => [
                'title' => 'ویجت اختصاصی المنتور (Elementor)',
                'desc'  => 'ابزارک هوشمند تولید محتوا مستقیماً در پنل طراحی المنتور.',
            ],
            'woocommerce' => [
                'title' => 'تولید محتوای فروشگاهی ووکامرس',
                'desc'  => 'تولید هوشمند توضیحات محصول، چکیده و ویژگی‌های سئو ووکامرس.',
            ],
            'chatbot' => [
                'title' => 'چت‌بات هوشمند تعاملی',
                'desc'  => 'دستیار شناور هوش مصنوعی برای پاسخ‌گویی به سوالات کاربران بر اساس سایت.',
            ],
            'bulk_generator' => [
                'title' => 'تولید انبوه مقالات (Bulk Generator)',
                'desc'  => 'تولید دسته‌ای نوشته‌ها در صف پس‌زمینه با Action Scheduler بدون تایم‌اوت.',
            ],
        ];

        foreach ($all as $id => $module) {
            $is_active = isset($states[$id]) ? (bool) $states[$id] : true;
            $title = ($is_fa && isset($fa_translations[$id])) ? $fa_translations[$id]['title'] : $module->get_title();
            $desc = ($is_fa && isset($fa_translations[$id])) ? $fa_translations[$id]['desc'] : $module->get_description();

            $output[] = [
                'id'          => $id,
                'title'       => $title,
                'description' => $desc,
                'active'      => $is_active,
            ];
        }

        return $output;
    }

    /**
     * Toggle module state.
     */
    public static function set_module_state(string $module_id, bool $active): void {
        $states = get_option('omni_ai_modules', []);
        $states[$module_id] = $active;
        update_option('omni_ai_modules', $states);
    }
}

### FILE: includes\providers\class-claude-provider.php
### SIZE: 7801 bytes
### PATH: I:\NextJS Projects\zebracode\zebracode\wordpress\plugins\omni-ai-suite\includes\providers\class-claude-provider.php
################################################################################

<?php
namespace OmniAI\Providers;

if (!defined('ABSPATH')) {
    exit;
}

/**
 * Anthropic Claude Provider implementation.
 */
class Claude_Provider implements AI_Provider_Interface {
    private string $api_key;
    private string $default_model;

    public function __construct(string $api_key = '', string $default_model = 'claude-3-5-sonnet-20241022') {
        $this->api_key = trim($api_key);
        $this->default_model = $default_model ?: 'claude-3-5-sonnet-20241022';
    }

    public function get_id(): string {
        return 'claude';
    }

    public function get_name(): string {
        return 'Anthropic Claude';
    }

    public function is_configured(): bool {
        return !empty($this->api_key);
    }

    public function get_supported_models(): array {
        return [
            'claude-3-5-sonnet-20241022' => 'Claude 3.5 Sonnet v2 (پیشنهادی - هوشمندترین مدل نگارش و کدنویسی)',
            'claude-3-5-haiku-20241022'  => 'Claude 3.5 Haiku (فوق سریع و سبک با درک بالا)',
            'claude-3-opus-20240229'     => 'Claude 3 Opus (تحلیل عمیق و تخصصی مقالات طولانی)',
            'claude-3-sonnet-20240229'   => 'Claude 3 Sonnet (نسخه استاندارد ۳)',
            'claude-3-haiku-20240307'    => 'Claude 3 Haiku (نسخه اقتصادی ۳)',
        ];
    }

    public function fetch_live_models(): array {
        if (!$this->is_configured()) {
            return $this->get_supported_models();
        }

        $response = wp_remote_get('https://api.anthropic.com/v1/models', [
            'headers' => [
                'x-api-key'         => $this->api_key,
                'anthropic-version' => '2023-06-01',
            ],
            'timeout' => 15,
        ]);

        if (is_wp_error($response) || wp_remote_retrieve_response_code($response) !== 200) {
            return $this->get_supported_models();
        }

        $body = json_decode(wp_remote_retrieve_body($response), true);
        if (empty($body['data']) || !is_array($body['data'])) {
            return $this->get_supported_models();
        }

        $models = [];
        $known = $this->get_supported_models();
        foreach ($body['data'] as $m) {
            $id = $m['id'] ?? '';
            $name = $m['display_name'] ?? $id;
            if (!empty($id)) {
                $models[$id] = $known[$id] ?? "{$name} ({$id})";
            }
        }

        return !empty($models) ? $models : $this->get_supported_models();
    }

    public function generate(string $prompt, array $options = []): array {
        if (!$this->is_configured()) {
            throw new \Exception(__('Claude API key is not configured.', 'omni-ai-suite'));
        }

        $model = $options['model'] ?? $this->default_model;
        $url = 'https://api.anthropic.com/v1/messages';

        $payload = [
            'model'      => $model,
            'max_tokens' => intval($options['max_tokens'] ?? 2048),
            'messages'   => [
                ['role' => 'user', 'content' => $prompt],
            ],
        ];

        if (!empty($options['system_prompt'])) {
            $payload['system'] = $options['system_prompt'];
        }

        $response = wp_remote_post($url, [
            'headers' => [
                'Content-Type'      => 'application/json',
                'x-api-key'         => $this->api_key,
                'anthropic-version' => '2023-06-01',
            ],
            'body'    => wp_json_encode($payload),
            'timeout' => 45,
        ]);

        if (is_wp_error($response)) {
            throw new \Exception($response->get_error_message());
        }

        $code = wp_remote_retrieve_response_code($response);
        $body = json_decode(wp_remote_retrieve_body($response), true);

        if ($code !== 200) {
            $msg = $body['error']['message'] ?? __('Failed to generate content from Claude API.', 'omni-ai-suite');
            throw new \Exception("Claude API Error ($code): $msg");
        }

        $text = '';
        if (!empty($body['content']) && is_array($body['content'])) {
            foreach ($body['content'] as $block) {
                if (($block['type'] ?? '') === 'text') {
                    $text .= $block['text'] ?? '';
                }
            }
        }

        $tokens = ($body['usage']['input_tokens'] ?? 0) + ($body['usage']['output_tokens'] ?? 0);

        return [
            'text'     => $text,
            'tokens'   => $tokens,
            'model'    => $model,
            'provider' => $this->get_id(),
        ];
    }

    public function stream(string $prompt, array $options = [], ?callable $chunk_callback = null): void {
        if (!$this->is_configured()) {
            throw new \Exception(__('Claude API key is not configured.', 'omni-ai-suite'));
        }

        $model = $options['model'] ?? $this->default_model;
        $url = 'https://api.anthropic.com/v1/messages';

        $payload = [
            'model'      => $model,
            'max_tokens' => intval($options['max_tokens'] ?? 2048),
            'messages'   => [
                ['role' => 'user', 'content' => $prompt],
            ],
            'stream'     => true,
        ];

        if (!empty($options['system_prompt'])) {
            $payload['system'] = $options['system_prompt'];
        }

        $ch = curl_init();
        curl_setopt($ch, CURLOPT_URL, $url);
        curl_setopt($ch, CURLOPT_POST, true);
        curl_setopt($ch, CURLOPT_POSTFIELDS, wp_json_encode($payload));
        curl_setopt($ch, CURLOPT_HTTPHEADER, [
            'Content-Type: application/json',
            'x-api-key: ' . $this->api_key,
            'anthropic-version: 2023-06-01',
        ]);
        curl_setopt($ch, CURLOPT_RETURNTRANSFER, false);
        curl_setopt($ch, CURLOPT_TIMEOUT, 120);
        curl_setopt($ch, CURLOPT_SSL_VERIFYPEER, true);

        $buffer = '';
        curl_setopt($ch, CURLOPT_WRITEFUNCTION, function ($ch, $data) use (&$buffer, $chunk_callback) {
            $buffer .= $data;
            while (($pos = strpos($buffer, "\n")) !== false) {
                $line = trim(substr($buffer, 0, $pos));
                $buffer = substr($buffer, $pos + 1);

                if (str_starts_with($line, 'data:')) {
                    $json_str = trim(substr($line, 5));
                    if (!empty($json_str)) {
                        $parsed = json_decode($json_str, true);
                        // Claude sends event 'content_block_delta'
                        if (($parsed['type'] ?? '') === 'content_block_delta') {
                            $chunk_text = $parsed['delta']['text'] ?? '';
                            if ($chunk_text !== '') {
                                if ($chunk_callback) {
                                    call_user_func($chunk_callback, $chunk_text);
                                } else {
                                    echo "data: " . wp_json_encode(['text' => $chunk_text, 'done' => false]) . "\n\n";
                                    if (ob_get_level() > 0) {
                                        ob_flush();
                                    }
                                    flush();
                                }
                            }
                        }
                    }
                }
            }
            return strlen($data);
        });

        $success = curl_exec($ch);
        $http_code = curl_getinfo($ch, CURLINFO_HTTP_CODE);
        $error = curl_error($ch);
        curl_close($ch);

        if (!$success || $http_code >= 400) {
            $err_msg = $error ? $error : "HTTP status $http_code";
            throw new \Exception("Claude streaming failed: $err_msg");
        }
    }
}

<?php
namespace OmniAI\Providers;

if (!defined('ABSPATH')) {
    exit;
}

/**
 * Google Gemini Provider implementation with automatic API version & model fallback.
 */
class Gemini_Provider implements AI_Provider_Interface {
    private string $api_key;
    private string $default_model;

    public function __construct(string $api_key = '', string $default_model = 'gemini-2.5-flash') {
        $this->api_key = trim($api_key);
        $this->default_model = $default_model ?: 'gemini-2.5-flash';
    }

    public function get_id(): string {
        return 'gemini';
    }

    public function get_name(): string {
        return 'Google Gemini';
    }

    public function is_configured(): bool {
        return !empty($this->api_key);
    }

    public function get_supported_models(): array {
        return [
            'gemini-2.5-flash'          => 'Gemini 2.5 Flash (پایدار، بسیار سریع و ۱ میلیون توکن - پیشنهادی)',
            'gemini-2.5-pro'            => 'Gemini 2.5 Pro (استدلال تخصصی و مقاله‌نویسی جامع)',
            'gemini-2.5-flash-lite'     => 'Gemini 2.5 Flash-Lite (فوق سریع و سبک)',
            'gemini-flash-latest'       => 'Gemini Flash Latest (جدیدترین نسخه فلش)',
            'gemini-pro-latest'         => 'Gemini Pro Latest (جدیدترین نسخه پرو)',
            'gemini-flash-lite-latest'  => 'Gemini Flash-Lite Latest (جدیدترین نسخه فلش لایت)',
            'gemini-3.8-flash'          => 'Gemini 3.8 Flash (نسل ۳.۸ پرسرعت)',
            'gemini-3.7-flash'          => 'Gemini 3.7 Flash (نسل ۳.۷ فلش)',
            'gemini-3.6-flash'          => 'Gemini 3.6 Flash (نسل ۳.۶ فلش)',
            'gemini-3.5-flash'          => 'Gemini 3.5 Flash (نسل ۳.۵ فلش)',
            'gemini-3.5-flash-lite'     => 'Gemini 3.5 Flash Lite (سبک نسل ۳.۵)',
            'gemini-3-flash-preview'    => 'Gemini 3 Flash Preview (پیش‌نمایش نسل ۳)',
            'gemini-3.1-pro-preview'    => 'Gemini 3.1 Pro Preview (استدلال پیشرفته)',
            'gemini-3.1-flash-lite'     => 'Gemini 3.1 Flash Lite (پیش‌نمایش سبک ۳.۱)',
            'gemini-4-argon'            => 'Gemini 4 Argon (نسل ۴ پیشرفته)',
            'gemma-4-31b-it'            => 'Gemma 4 31B IT (مدل متنی باز گما)',
            'gemma-4-26b-a4b-it'        => 'Gemma 4 26B A4B IT (مدل سبک گما)',
        ];
    }

    public function fetch_live_models(): array {
        if (!$this->is_configured()) {
            return $this->get_supported_models();
        }

        $endpoints = [
            'v1beta' => "https://generativelanguage.googleapis.com/v1beta/models?key=" . rawurlencode($this->api_key),
            'v1'     => "https://generativelanguage.googleapis.com/v1/models?key=" . rawurlencode($this->api_key),
        ];

        $models = [];
        $known = $this->get_supported_models();

        foreach ($endpoints as $version => $url) {
            $raw_models = $this->fetch_models_from_url($url);
            foreach ($raw_models as $m) {
                $methods = $m['supportedGenerationMethods'] ?? [];
                if (!is_array($methods) || !in_array('generateContent', $methods, true)) {
                    continue;
                }

                $slug = str_replace('models/', '', $m['name']);

                // Only include Gemini and Gemma content/text generation models
                if (!str_starts_with($slug, 'gemini-') && !str_starts_with($slug, 'gemma-')) {
                    continue;
                }

                // Strictly filter out all image generation models (e.g. imagen, gemini-*-image, banana, etc.)
                $is_image = (
                    str_contains($slug, 'image') ||
                    str_contains($slug, 'imagen') ||
                    str_contains($slug, 'banana') ||
                    str_contains($slug, 'picture') ||
                    str_contains($slug, 'photo') ||
                    str_contains($slug, 'draw') ||
                    str_contains($slug, 'diffusion') ||
                    str_contains($slug, 'flux')
                );
                if ($is_image) {
                    continue;
                }

                // Strictly filter out audio, speech, TTS, video, embedding, and non-content models
                $is_non_content = (
                    str_contains($slug, 'embedding') ||
                    str_contains($slug, 'aqa') ||
                    str_contains($slug, 'audio') ||
                    str_contains($slug, 'speech') ||
                    str_contains($slug, 'tts') ||
                    str_contains($slug, 'transcribe') ||
                    str_contains($slug, 'whisper') ||
                    str_contains($slug, 'realtime') ||
                    str_contains($slug, 'lyria') ||
                    str_contains($slug, 'veo') ||
                    str_contains($slug, 'video') ||
                    str_contains($slug, 'robotics') ||
                    str_contains($slug, 'computer-use') ||
                    str_contains($slug, 'antigravity') ||
                    str_contains($slug, 'deep-research')
                );
                if ($is_non_content) {
                    continue;
                }

                // Filter out dated snapshot duplicates (like -001, -002, etc.) to keep clean list
                if (preg_match('/-\d{3}$/', $slug) || preg_match('/-\d{4}$/', $slug)) {
                    continue;
                }

                if (!isset($models[$slug])) {
                    if (isset($known[$slug])) {
                        $models[$slug] = $known[$slug];
                    } else {
                        $displayName = !empty($m['displayName']) ? $m['displayName'] : $slug;
                        if (str_contains($slug, 'exp') || str_contains($slug, 'preview')) {
                            $models[$slug] = "{$displayName} (پیش‌نمایش - {$version})";
                        } else {
                            $models[$slug] = "{$displayName} ({$version})";
                        }
                    }
                }
            }
        }

        // Ensure primary models are guaranteed available in the list
        if (!isset($models['gemini-2.5-flash'])) {
            $models = array_merge(['gemini-2.5-flash' => 'Gemini 2.5 Flash (پایدار، بسیار سریع و ۱ میلیون توکن - پیشنهادی)'], $models);
        }

        return !empty($models) ? $models : $this->get_supported_models();
    }

    private function fetch_models_from_url(string $url): array {
        $body_str = '';
        if (function_exists('curl_init')) {
            $ch = curl_init();
            curl_setopt($ch, CURLOPT_URL, $url);
            curl_setopt($ch, CURLOPT_RETURNTRANSFER, true);
            curl_setopt($ch, CURLOPT_CONNECTTIMEOUT, 10);
            curl_setopt($ch, CURLOPT_TIMEOUT, 15);
            curl_setopt($ch, CURLOPT_SSL_VERIFYPEER, apply_filters('https_ssl_verify', true));
            curl_setopt($ch, CURLOPT_HTTPHEADER, [
                'Content-Type: application/json',
                'Accept: application/json',
            ]);
            $body_str = curl_exec($ch);
            $http_code = curl_getinfo($ch, CURLINFO_HTTP_CODE);
            curl_close($ch);

            if ($http_code !== 200 || empty($body_str)) {
                $body_str = '';
            }
        }

        if (empty($body_str)) {
            $response = wp_remote_get($url, [
                'timeout'   => 15,
                'sslverify' => apply_filters('https_ssl_verify', true),
                'headers'   => [
                    'Accept' => 'application/json',
                ],
            ]);
            if (!is_wp_error($response) && wp_remote_retrieve_response_code($response) === 200) {
                $body_str = wp_remote_retrieve_body($response);
            }
        }

        if (empty($body_str)) {
            return [];
        }

        $decoded = json_decode($body_str, true);
        return (!empty($decoded['models']) && is_array($decoded['models'])) ? $decoded['models'] : [];
    }

    private function get_clean_model(string $model): string {
        $model = preg_replace('/^models\//', '', trim($model));
        return $model ?: $this->default_model;
    }

    /**
     * Build list of candidate API versions (v1beta, v1) for the requested model with resilient fallback.
     */
    private function get_request_candidates(string $model): array {
        $clean = $this->get_clean_model($model);
        $candidates = [];

        // Primary candidate on v1beta
        $candidates[] = ['version' => 'v1beta', 'model' => $clean];

        // If gemini-3.7-flash (deprecated in favor of 3.8-flash by Google) or 3.8-flash,
        // provide intelligent fallback to avoid halting user requests with 400
        if ($clean === 'gemini-3.7-flash') {
            $candidates[] = ['version' => 'v1beta', 'model' => 'gemini-3.8-flash'];
            $candidates[] = ['version' => 'v1beta', 'model' => 'gemini-3.6-flash'];
        } elseif ($clean === 'gemini-3.8-flash') {
            $candidates[] = ['version' => 'v1beta', 'model' => 'gemini-3.6-flash'];
        }

        // Also try v1 for standard release models
        $candidates[] = ['version' => 'v1', 'model' => $clean];

        return $candidates;
    }

    public function generate(string $prompt, array $options = []): array {
        if (!$this->is_configured()) {
            throw new \Exception(__('Gemini API key is not configured. Please add it in settings or options-connectors.php.', 'omni-ai-suite'));
        }

        $input_model = $options['model'] ?? $this->default_model;
        $clean = $this->get_clean_model($input_model);
        $candidates = $this->get_request_candidates($input_model);

        $gen_config = [
            'temperature'     => floatval($options['temperature'] ?? 0.7),
            'maxOutputTokens' => !empty($options['max_tokens']) ? max(intval($options['max_tokens']), 4096) : 8192,
        ];

        // Configure thinking level for Gemini 3.x / 4.x reasoning models
        if (str_contains($clean, '3.') || str_contains($clean, '3-') || str_contains($clean, '4-')) {
            $gen_config['thinkingConfig'] = [
                'thinkingLevel' => 'LOW',
            ];
        }

        $payload = [
            'contents' => [
                [
                    'role'  => 'user',
                    'parts' => [['text' => $prompt]],
                ],
            ],
            'generationConfig' => $gen_config,
        ];

        if (!empty($options['system_prompt'])) {
            $payload['systemInstruction'] = [
                'parts' => [['text' => $options['system_prompt']]],
            ];
        }

        $ssl_verify = apply_filters('https_ssl_verify', true);
        $last_error = '';

        foreach ($candidates as $cand) {
            $ver = $cand['version'];
            $mod = $cand['model'];
            $mod_clean = preg_replace('/^models\//', '', $mod);
            $url = "https://generativelanguage.googleapis.com/{$ver}/models/{$mod_clean}:generateContent?key=" . urlencode($this->api_key);

            $response = wp_remote_post($url, [
                'headers' => [
                    'Content-Type'   => 'application/json',
                    'X-goog-api-key' => $this->api_key,
                    'Accept'         => 'application/json',
                ],
                'body'      => wp_json_encode($payload),
                'timeout'   => 45,
                'sslverify' => $ssl_verify,
            ]);

            if (is_wp_error($response)) {
                $last_error = $response->get_error_message();
                continue;
            }

            $code = wp_remote_retrieve_response_code($response);
            $raw_body = wp_remote_retrieve_body($response);
            $body = json_decode($raw_body, true);

            if ($code === 200 && !empty($body['candidates'][0]['content']['parts']) && is_array($body['candidates'][0]['content']['parts'])) {
                $text = '';
                foreach ($body['candidates'][0]['content']['parts'] as $part) {
                    if (empty($part['thought']) && isset($part['text'])) {
                        $text .= $part['text'];
                    }
                }
                if ($text === '') {
                    foreach ($body['candidates'][0]['content']['parts'] as $part) {
                        if (isset($part['text'])) {
                            $text .= $part['text'];
                        }
                    }
                }

                if ($text !== '') {
                    $tokens = $body['usageMetadata']['totalTokenCount'] ?? 0;
                    return [
                        'text'     => $text,
                        'tokens'   => $tokens,
                        'model'    => $mod,
                        'provider' => $this->get_id(),
                    ];
                }
            }

            // If error, continue to next candidate unless invalid key (401/403)
            $last_error = $body['error']['message'] ?? "HTTP $code: " . substr(strip_tags($raw_body), 0, 200);
            if ($code === 401 || $code === 403) {
                break;
            }
        }

        throw new \Exception("Gemini API Error: " . $last_error);
    }

    public function stream(string $prompt, array $options = [], ?callable $chunk_callback = null): void {
        @set_time_limit(300);
        @ini_set('max_execution_time', '300');

        if (!$this->is_configured()) {
            throw new \Exception(__('Gemini API key is not configured. Please add it in settings or options-connectors.php.', 'omni-ai-suite'));
        }

        $input_model = $options['model'] ?? $this->default_model;
        $clean = $this->get_clean_model($input_model);
        $candidates = $this->get_request_candidates($input_model);

        $gen_config = [
            'temperature'     => floatval($options['temperature'] ?? 0.7),
            'maxOutputTokens' => !empty($options['max_tokens']) ? max(intval($options['max_tokens']), 4096) : 8192,
        ];

        // Configure thinking level for Gemini 3.x / 4.x reasoning models
        if (str_contains($clean, '3.') || str_contains($clean, '3-') || str_contains($clean, '4-')) {
            $gen_config['thinkingConfig'] = [
                'thinkingLevel' => 'LOW',
            ];
        }

        $payload = [
            'contents' => [
                [
                    'role'  => 'user',
                    'parts' => [['text' => $prompt]],
                ],
            ],
            'generationConfig' => $gen_config,
        ];

        if (!empty($options['system_prompt'])) {
            $payload['systemInstruction'] = [
                'parts' => [['text' => $options['system_prompt']]],
            ];
        }

        $ssl_verify = apply_filters('https_ssl_verify', true);
        $last_stream_err = '';

        foreach ($candidates as $cand) {
            $ver = $cand['version'];
            $mod = $cand['model'];
            $mod_clean = preg_replace('/^models\//', '', $mod);
            $url = "https://generativelanguage.googleapis.com/{$ver}/models/{$mod_clean}:streamGenerateContent?alt=sse&key=" . urlencode($this->api_key);

            $ch = curl_init();
            curl_setopt($ch, CURLOPT_URL, $url);
            curl_setopt($ch, CURLOPT_POST, true);
            curl_setopt($ch, CURLOPT_POSTFIELDS, wp_json_encode($payload));
            curl_setopt($ch, CURLOPT_HTTPHEADER, [
                'Content-Type: application/json',
                'X-goog-api-key: ' . $this->api_key,
            ]);
            curl_setopt($ch, CURLOPT_RETURNTRANSFER, false);
            curl_setopt($ch, CURLOPT_CONNECTTIMEOUT, 15);
            curl_setopt($ch, CURLOPT_TIMEOUT, 180);

            if (defined('CURLOPT_IPRESOLVE') && defined('CURL_IPRESOLVE_V4')) {
                curl_setopt($ch, CURLOPT_IPRESOLVE, CURL_IPRESOLVE_V4);
            }

            curl_setopt($ch, CURLOPT_SSL_VERIFYPEER, $ssl_verify);
            curl_setopt($ch, CURLOPT_SSL_VERIFYHOST, $ssl_verify ? 2 : 0);

            if (defined('WP_PROXY_HOST')) {
                curl_setopt($ch, CURLOPT_PROXY, WP_PROXY_HOST);
                if (defined('WP_PROXY_PORT')) {
                    curl_setopt($ch, CURLOPT_PROXYPORT, WP_PROXY_PORT);
                }
                if (defined('WP_PROXY_USERNAME') && defined('WP_PROXY_PASSWORD')) {
                    curl_setopt($ch, CURLOPT_PROXYUSERPWD, WP_PROXY_USERNAME . ':' . WP_PROXY_PASSWORD);
                }
            }

            $buffer = '';
            $raw_response = '';
            $has_chunks = false;

            curl_setopt($ch, CURLOPT_WRITEFUNCTION, function ($ch, $data) use (&$buffer, &$raw_response, &$has_chunks, $chunk_callback) {
                $raw_response .= $data;
                $buffer .= $data;

                while (($pos = strpos($buffer, "\n")) !== false) {
                    $line = trim(substr($buffer, 0, $pos));
                    $buffer = substr($buffer, $pos + 1);

                    if ($line === '' || $line === '[' || $line === ']' || $line === ',') {
                        continue;
                    }

                    $json_str = '';
                    if (str_starts_with($line, 'data:')) {
                        $json_str = trim(substr($line, 5));
                    } elseif (str_starts_with($line, '{')) {
                        $json_str = rtrim($line, ',');
                    }

                    if (!empty($json_str)) {
                        $parsed = json_decode($json_str, true);
                        if (!empty($parsed['error']['message'])) {
                            return strlen($data);
                        }

                        $chunk_text = '';
                        if (!empty($parsed['candidates'][0]['content']['parts']) && is_array($parsed['candidates'][0]['content']['parts'])) {
                            foreach ($parsed['candidates'][0]['content']['parts'] as $part) {
                                // Mark connection as active even when reasoning
                                if (!empty($part['thought'])) {
                                    $has_chunks = true;
                                    continue;
                                }
                                if (isset($part['text']) && $part['text'] !== '') {
                                    $chunk_text .= $part['text'];
                                }
                            }
                        }

                        if ($chunk_text !== '') {
                            $has_chunks = true;
                            if ($chunk_callback) {
                                call_user_func($chunk_callback, $chunk_text);
                            } else {
                                echo "data: " . wp_json_encode(['text' => $chunk_text, 'done' => false]) . "\n\n";
                                if (ob_get_level() > 0) {
                                    @ob_flush();
                                }
                                @flush();
                            }
                        }
                    }
                }
                return strlen($data);
            });

            $success = curl_exec($ch);
            $http_code = curl_getinfo($ch, CURLINFO_HTTP_CODE);
            $curl_err = curl_error($ch);
            curl_close($ch);

            // Flush any remaining trailing buffer chunk
            if (!empty($buffer)) {
                $line = trim($buffer);
                $json_str = '';
                if (str_starts_with($line, 'data:')) {
                    $json_str = trim(substr($line, 5));
                } elseif (str_starts_with($line, '{')) {
                    $json_str = rtrim($line, ',');
                }
                if (!empty($json_str)) {
                    $parsed = json_decode($json_str, true);
                    $chunk_text = '';
                    if (!empty($parsed['candidates'][0]['content']['parts']) && is_array($parsed['candidates'][0]['content']['parts'])) {
                        foreach ($parsed['candidates'][0]['content']['parts'] as $part) {
                            if (!empty($part['thought'])) {
                                $has_chunks = true;
                                continue;
                            }
                            if (isset($part['text']) && $part['text'] !== '') {
                                $chunk_text .= $part['text'];
                            }
                        }
                    }
                    if ($chunk_text !== '') {
                        $has_chunks = true;
                        if ($chunk_callback) {
                            call_user_func($chunk_callback, $chunk_text);
                        } else {
                            echo "data: " . wp_json_encode(['text' => $chunk_text, 'done' => false]) . "\n\n";
                            if (ob_get_level() > 0) {
                                @ob_flush();
                            }
                            @flush();
                        }
                    }
                }
            }

            if ($has_chunks) {
                // Successfully streamed content to client!
                return;
            }

            if (!empty($raw_response)) {
                $decoded = json_decode($raw_response, true);
                if (!empty($decoded['error']['message'])) {
                    $last_stream_err = $decoded['error']['message'];
                }
            }
            if (empty($last_stream_err)) {
                $last_stream_err = $curl_err ?: "HTTP $http_code";
            }

            // Only abort early on authorization or forbidden errors
            if ($http_code === 401 || $http_code === 403) {
                break;
            }
        }

        throw new \Exception("Gemini API Error: " . $last_stream_err);
    }
}
