<?php
namespace OmniAI\Providers;

if (!defined('ABSPATH')) {
    exit;
}

/**
 * OpenAI Provider implementation (GPT-4o, GPT-4o-mini, etc.).
 */
class OpenAI_Provider implements AI_Provider_Interface {
    private string $api_key;
    private string $default_model;

    public function __construct(string $api_key = '', string $default_model = 'gpt-4o-mini') {
        $this->api_key = trim($api_key);
        $this->default_model = $default_model ?: 'gpt-4o-mini';
    }

    public function get_id(): string {
        return 'openai';
    }

    public function get_name(): string {
        return 'OpenAI';
    }

    public function is_configured(): bool {
        return !empty($this->api_key);
    }

    public function get_supported_models(): array {
        return [
            'gpt-4o-mini'       => 'GPT-4o Mini (سریع، مقرون‌به‌صرفه و هوشمند - پیشنهادی)',
            'gpt-4o'            => 'GPT-4o (پرچمدار هوشمند همه‌منظوره)',
            'gpt-4o-2024-11-20' => 'GPT-4o (آخرین نسخه پایدار نوامبر)',
            'chatgpt-4o-latest' => 'ChatGPT-4o (نسخه داینامیک چت‌جی‌پی‌تی)',
            'o3-mini'           => 'o3-mini (جدیدترین مدل استدلال تخصصی و کدنویسی)',
            'o1-mini'           => 'o1-mini (مدل استدلال ریاضی و سریع)',
            'o1-preview'        => 'o1-preview (مدل استدلال و تحلیل عمیق)',
            'gpt-4-turbo'       => 'GPT-4 Turbo (مدل ۱۲۸ هزار توکن)',
            'gpt-4'             => 'GPT-4 (نسخه استاندارد ۴)',
            'gpt-3.5-turbo'     => 'GPT-3.5 Turbo (مدل اقتصادی قدیمی)',
        ];
    }

    public function fetch_live_models(): array {
        if (!$this->is_configured()) {
            return $this->get_supported_models();
        }

        $response = wp_remote_get('https://api.openai.com/v1/models', [
            'headers' => [
                'Authorization' => 'Bearer ' . $this->api_key,
            ],
            'timeout' => 15,
        ]);

        if (is_wp_error($response) || wp_remote_retrieve_response_code($response) !== 200) {
            return $this->get_supported_models();
        }

        $body = json_decode(wp_remote_retrieve_body($response), true);
        if (empty($body['data']) || !is_array($body['data'])) {
            return $this->get_supported_models();
        }

        $models = [];
        $known_labels = $this->get_supported_models();

        foreach ($body['data'] as $m) {
            $id = $m['id'] ?? '';
            // Only text/content generation models - strictly exclude image (dall-e), audio, and embedding models
            if (
                preg_match('/^(gpt-4|gpt-3\.5|o1|o3|chatgpt)/i', $id) &&
                !str_contains($id, 'audio') &&
                !str_contains($id, 'realtime') &&
                !str_contains($id, 'image') &&
                !str_contains($id, 'dall-e') &&
                !str_contains($id, 'transcribe') &&
                !str_contains($id, 'embedding')
            ) {
                $label = $known_labels[$id] ?? "OpenAI {$id}";
                $models[$id] = $label;
            }
        }

        uksort($models, function ($a, $b) {
            return strcmp($b, $a);
        });

        return !empty($models) ? $models : $this->get_supported_models();
    }

    public function generate(string $prompt, array $options = []): array {
        if (!$this->is_configured()) {
            throw new \Exception(__('OpenAI API key is not configured.', 'omni-ai-suite'));
        }

        $model = $options['model'] ?? $this->default_model;
        $url = 'https://api.openai.com/v1/chat/completions';

        $messages = [];
        if (!empty($options['system_prompt'])) {
            $messages[] = ['role' => 'system', 'content' => $options['system_prompt']];
        }
        $messages[] = ['role' => 'user', 'content' => $prompt];

        $payload = [
            'model'       => $model,
            'messages'    => $messages,
            'temperature' => floatval($options['temperature'] ?? 0.7),
            'max_tokens'  => intval($options['max_tokens'] ?? 2048),
        ];

        $response = wp_remote_post($url, [
            'headers' => [
                'Content-Type'  => 'application/json',
                'Authorization' => 'Bearer ' . $this->api_key,
            ],
            'body'    => wp_json_encode($payload),
            'timeout' => 45,
        ]);

        if (is_wp_error($response)) {
            throw new \Exception($response->get_error_message());
        }

        $code = wp_remote_retrieve_response_code($response);
        $body = json_decode(wp_remote_retrieve_body($response), true);

        if ($code !== 200) {
            $msg = $body['error']['message'] ?? __('Failed to generate content from OpenAI API.', 'omni-ai-suite');
            throw new \Exception("OpenAI API Error ($code): $msg");
        }

        $text = $body['choices'][0]['message']['content'] ?? '';
        $tokens = $body['usage']['total_tokens'] ?? 0;

        return [
            'text'     => $text,
            'tokens'   => $tokens,
            'model'    => $model,
            'provider' => $this->get_id(),
        ];
    }

    public function stream(string $prompt, array $options = [], ?callable $chunk_callback = null): void {
        if (!$this->is_configured()) {
            throw new \Exception(__('OpenAI API key is not configured.', 'omni-ai-suite'));
        }

        $model = $options['model'] ?? $this->default_model;
        $url = 'https://api.openai.com/v1/chat/completions';

        $messages = [];
        if (!empty($options['system_prompt'])) {
            $messages[] = ['role' => 'system', 'content' => $options['system_prompt']];
        }
        $messages[] = ['role' => 'user', 'content' => $prompt];

        $payload = [
            'model'       => $model,
            'messages'    => $messages,
            'temperature' => floatval($options['temperature'] ?? 0.7),
            'max_tokens'  => intval($options['max_tokens'] ?? 2048),
            'stream'      => true,
        ];

        $ch = curl_init();
        curl_setopt($ch, CURLOPT_URL, $url);
        curl_setopt($ch, CURLOPT_POST, true);
        curl_setopt($ch, CURLOPT_POSTFIELDS, wp_json_encode($payload));
        curl_setopt($ch, CURLOPT_HTTPHEADER, [
            'Content-Type: application/json',
            'Authorization: Bearer ' . $this->api_key,
        ]);
        curl_setopt($ch, CURLOPT_RETURNTRANSFER, false);
        curl_setopt($ch, CURLOPT_TIMEOUT, 120);
        curl_setopt($ch, CURLOPT_SSL_VERIFYPEER, true);

        $buffer = '';
        curl_setopt($ch, CURLOPT_WRITEFUNCTION, function ($ch, $data) use (&$buffer, $chunk_callback) {
            $buffer .= $data;
            while (($pos = strpos($buffer, "\n")) !== false) {
                $line = trim(substr($buffer, 0, $pos));
                $buffer = substr($buffer, $pos + 1);

                if (str_starts_with($line, 'data:')) {
                    $json_str = trim(substr($line, 5));
                    if ($json_str === '[DONE]') {
                        continue;
                    }
                    if (!empty($json_str)) {
                        $parsed = json_decode($json_str, true);
                        $chunk_text = $parsed['choices'][0]['delta']['content'] ?? '';
                        if ($chunk_text !== '') {
                            if ($chunk_callback) {
                                call_user_func($chunk_callback, $chunk_text);
                            } else {
                                echo "data: " . wp_json_encode(['text' => $chunk_text, 'done' => false]) . "\n\n";
                                if (ob_get_level() > 0) {
                                    ob_flush();
                                }
                                flush();
                            }
                        }
                    }
                }
            }
            return strlen($data);
        });

        $success = curl_exec($ch);
        $http_code = curl_getinfo($ch, CURLINFO_HTTP_CODE);
        $error = curl_error($ch);
        curl_close($ch);

        if (!$success || $http_code >= 400) {
            $err_msg = $error ? $error : "HTTP status $http_code";
            throw new \Exception("OpenAI streaming failed: $err_msg");
        }
    }
}

### FILE: includes\providers\class-provider-factory.php

<?php
namespace OmniAI\Providers;

if (!defined('ABSPATH')) {
    exit;
}

/**
 * Service Provider Factory & Registry.
 * Deeply integrates with WordPress Core Connectors API (wp-admin/options-connectors.php)
 * to automatically reuse API keys configured across the site.
 */
class Provider_Factory {
    private static ?array $providers = null;

    /**
     * Get instance of a specific provider or default configured provider.
     */
    public static function get_provider(?string $provider_id = null): AI_Provider_Interface {
        $providers = self::get_all_providers();
        $settings = self::get_settings();

        if (empty($provider_id)) {
            $provider_id = $settings['default_provider'] ?? 'gemini';
        }

        if (isset($providers[$provider_id])) {
            return $providers[$provider_id];
        }

        // Return first configured provider if default is invalid
        foreach ($providers as $provider) {
            if ($provider->is_configured()) {
                return $provider;
            }
        }

        // Return default provider even if unconfigured (it will throw clean error upon invocation)
        return $providers['gemini'] ?? reset($providers);
    }

    /**
     * Retrieve all instantiated providers based on saved settings & core connectors.
     *
     * @return array<string, AI_Provider_Interface>
     */
    public static function get_all_providers(): array {
        if (self::$providers !== null) {
            return self::$providers;
        }

        $settings = self::get_settings();

        self::$providers = [
            'gemini' => new Gemini_Provider(
                $settings['gemini_api_key'] ?? '',
                $settings['gemini_model'] ?? 'gemini-2.5-flash'
            ),
            'openai' => new OpenAI_Provider(
                $settings['openai_api_key'] ?? '',
                $settings['openai_model'] ?? 'gpt-4o-mini'
            ),
            'claude' => new Claude_Provider(
                $settings['claude_api_key'] ?? '',
                $settings['claude_model'] ?? 'claude-3-5-sonnet-20241022'
            ),
        ];

        return self::$providers;
    }

    /**
     * Resolve effective API key with WordPress Connectors (options-connectors.php) priority.
     * Priority order:
     * 1. WordPress Core Connectors API (wp_get_connector / options-connectors.php)
     * 2. Direct Core Connector options (connectors_ai_*_api_key)
     * 3. Constants / Env vars (GOOGLE_API_KEY, OPENAI_API_KEY, ANTHROPIC_API_KEY)
     * 4. Plugin's own custom settings (omni_ai_settings)
     *
     * @param string $provider 'gemini', 'openai', or 'claude'
     * @param string $legacy_key Custom key saved in plugin settings
     * @return array ['key' => string, 'source' => 'connector'|'constant'|'env'|'plugin'|'none']
     */
    public static function resolve_api_key(string $provider, string $legacy_key = ''): array {
        // 1. Check WordPress Core Connectors API registry (options-connectors.php)
        if (function_exists('wp_get_connector')) {
            switch ($provider) {
                case 'gemini':
                case 'google':
                    $connector_ids = ['google', 'gemini'];
                    break;
                case 'openai':
                    $connector_ids = ['openai', 'openai-compatible'];
                    break;
                case 'claude':
                case 'anthropic':
                    $connector_ids = ['anthropic', 'claude'];
                    break;
                default:
                    $connector_ids = [$provider];
                    break;
            }

            foreach ($connector_ids as $cid) {
                if (function_exists('wp_is_connector_registered') && !wp_is_connector_registered($cid)) {
                    continue;
                }
                $connector = wp_get_connector($cid);
                if (is_array($connector) && !empty($connector['authentication'])) {
                    $auth = $connector['authentication'];

                    // Check Env var from connector definition
                    if (!empty($auth['env_var_name'])) {
                        $env = getenv($auth['env_var_name']);
                        if ($env !== false && trim((string) $env) !== '') {
                            return ['key' => trim((string) $env), 'source' => 'env'];
                        }
                    }

                    // Check Constant from connector definition
                    if (!empty($auth['constant_name']) && defined($auth['constant_name'])) {
                        $cval = constant($auth['constant_name']);
                        if (is_scalar($cval) && trim((string) $cval) !== '') {
                            return ['key' => trim((string) $cval), 'source' => 'constant'];
                        }
                    }

                    // Check database option from connector definition
                    if (!empty($auth['setting_name'])) {
                        $opt_val = get_option($auth['setting_name'], '');
                        if (is_string($opt_val) && trim($opt_val) !== '') {
                            return ['key' => trim($opt_val), 'source' => 'connector'];
                        }
                    }
                }
            }
        }

        // 2. Direct database options created by options-connectors.php
        switch ($provider) {
            case 'gemini':
            case 'google':
                $direct_connector_options = [
                    'connectors_ai_google_api_key',
                    'connectors_google_api_key',
                    'connectors_ai_gemini_api_key',
                    'google_api_key',
                ];
                break;
            case 'openai':
                $direct_connector_options = [
                    'connectors_ai_openai_api_key',
                    'connectors_openai_api_key',
                    'openai_api_key',
                ];
                break;
            case 'claude':
            case 'anthropic':
                $direct_connector_options = [
                    'connectors_ai_anthropic_api_key',
                    'connectors_anthropic_api_key',
                    'anthropic_api_key',
                    'claude_api_key',
                ];
                break;
            default:
                $direct_connector_options = [];
                break;
        }

        foreach ($direct_connector_options as $opt_name) {
            $opt_val = get_option($opt_name, '');
            if (is_string($opt_val) && trim($opt_val) !== '') {
                return ['key' => trim($opt_val), 'source' => 'connector'];
            }
        }

        // 3. System constants / Environment variables
        switch ($provider) {
            case 'gemini':
            case 'google':
                $constants = ['GOOGLE_API_KEY', 'GEMINI_API_KEY'];
                break;
            case 'openai':
                $constants = ['OPENAI_API_KEY'];
                break;
            case 'claude':
            case 'anthropic':
                $constants = ['ANTHROPIC_API_KEY', 'CLAUDE_API_KEY'];
                break;
            default:
                $constants = [];
                break;
        }

        foreach ($constants as $const_name) {
            $env_val = getenv($const_name);
            if ($env_val !== false && trim((string) $env_val) !== '') {
                return ['key' => trim((string) $env_val), 'source' => 'env'];
            }
            if (defined($const_name)) {
                $const_val = constant($const_name);
                if (is_scalar($const_val) && trim((string) $const_val) !== '') {
                    return ['key' => trim((string) $const_val), 'source' => 'constant'];
                }
            }
        }

        // 4. Query wp_options directly for any matching connector setting
        global $wpdb;
        if (isset($wpdb)) {
            switch ($provider) {
                case 'gemini':
                case 'google':
                    $like_patterns = ['connectors%google%', 'connectors%gemini%', '%google%api_key%'];
                    break;
                case 'openai':
                    $like_patterns = ['connectors%openai%', '%openai%api_key%'];
                    break;
                case 'claude':
                case 'anthropic':
                    $like_patterns = ['connectors%anthropic%', 'connectors%claude%', '%claude%api_key%'];
                    break;
                default:
                    $like_patterns = [];
                    break;
            }
            foreach ($like_patterns as $pattern) {
                $found = $wpdb->get_var($wpdb->prepare(
                    "SELECT option_value FROM {$wpdb->options} WHERE option_name LIKE %s AND option_value != '' LIMIT 1",
                    $pattern
                ));
                if (!empty($found) && is_string($found) && strlen(trim($found)) > 10) {
                    return ['key' => trim($found), 'source' => 'connector'];
                }
            }
        }

        // 5. Fallback to custom plugin-stored key
        if (!empty($legacy_key) && trim($legacy_key) !== '') {
            return ['key' => trim($legacy_key), 'source' => 'plugin'];
        }

        return ['key' => '', 'source' => 'none'];
    }

    /**
     * Get settings merging plugin preferences with core WP Connectors keys.
     */
    public static function get_settings(): array {
        $stored = get_option('omni_ai_settings', []);
        if (!is_array($stored)) {
            $stored = [];
        }

        // Automatically resolve API keys from options-connectors.php
        $gemini_res = self::resolve_api_key('gemini', $stored['gemini_api_key'] ?? '');
        $openai_res = self::resolve_api_key('openai', $stored['openai_api_key'] ?? '');
        $claude_res = self::resolve_api_key('claude', $stored['claude_api_key'] ?? '');

        $stored['gemini_api_key'] = $gemini_res['key'];
        $stored['gemini_key_source'] = $gemini_res['source'];

        $stored['openai_api_key'] = $openai_res['key'];
        $stored['openai_key_source'] = $openai_res['source'];

        $stored['claude_api_key'] = $claude_res['key'];
        $stored['claude_key_source'] = $claude_res['source'];

        // Automatically pick configured default provider if current default has no key
        $default_provider = $stored['default_provider'] ?? 'gemini';
        if ($default_provider === 'gemini' && empty($stored['gemini_api_key'])) {
            if (!empty($stored['openai_api_key'])) {
                $default_provider = 'openai';
            } elseif (!empty($stored['claude_api_key'])) {
                $default_provider = 'claude';
            }
        }

        return wp_parse_args($stored, [
            'default_provider'       => $default_provider,
            'fallback_provider'      => $stored['fallback_provider'] ?? 'none',
            'default_image_provider' => $stored['default_image_provider'] ?? 'gemini',
            'gemini_api_key'         => '',
            'gemini_key_source'      => 'none',
            'gemini_model'           => 'gemini-1.5-flash',
            'openai_api_key'         => '',
            'openai_key_source'      => 'none',
            'openai_model'           => 'gpt-4o-mini',
            'claude_api_key'         => '',
            'claude_key_source'      => 'none',
            'claude_model'           => 'claude-3-5-sonnet-20241022',
            'temperature'            => 0.7,
            'max_tokens'             => 2000,
        ]);
    }

    /**
     * Reset cached provider instances (e.g. after saving options).
     */
    public static function reset(): void {
        self::$providers = null;
    }
}

<?php
namespace OmniAI\Providers;

if (!defined('ABSPATH')) {
    exit;
}

/**
 * Interface for AI service providers (Gemini, OpenAI, Claude, etc.)
 */
interface AI_Provider_Interface {
    /**
     * Unique identifier for the provider.
     */
    public function get_id(): string;

    /**
     * Human-readable name.
     */
    public function get_name(): string;

    /**
     * Check if provider has required credentials.
     */
    public function is_configured(): bool;

    /**
     * Get available models for this provider.
     *
     * @return array<string, string> Key is model slug, value is label.
     */
    public function get_supported_models(): array;

    /**
     * Fetch live list of active models directly from provider API.
     *
     * @return array<string, string> Key is model slug, value is label.
     */
    public function fetch_live_models(): array;

    /**
     * Synchronous content generation.
     *
     * @param string $prompt Prompt string.
     * @param array $options Model options (model, temperature, max_tokens, system_prompt, etc.)
     * @return array ['text' => string, 'tokens' => int, 'model' => string, 'provider' => string]
     * @throws \Exception On API error.
     */
    public function generate(string $prompt, array $options = []): array;

    /**
     * Streaming content generation via cURL and Server-Sent Events (SSE).
     *
     * @param string $prompt Prompt string.
     * @param array $options Model options.
     * @param callable|null $chunk_callback Optional callback called with each chunk string.
     * @return void
     * @throws \Exception On connection or authorization error.
     */
    public function stream(string $prompt, array $options = [], ?callable $chunk_callback = null): void;
}

<?php
/**
 * Plugin Name:       Omni AI Suite - All-in-One AI for WordPress
 * Plugin URI:        https://github.com/zebracode/omni-ai-suite
 * Description:       A modular, high-performance All-in-One WordPress AI plugin with BYOK, real-time streaming, Action Scheduler bulk generation, Gutenberg & Elementor integration, and zero DB bloat.
 * Version:           1.0.0
 * Requires at least: 6.4
 * Requires PHP:      8.0
 * Author:            Omni AI Team
 * Author URI:        https://zebracode.ir
 * License:           GPL v2 or later
 * License URI:       https://www.gnu.org/licenses/gpl-2.0.html
 * Text Domain:       omni-ai-suite
 * Domain Path:       /languages
 */

if (!defined('ABSPATH')) {
    exit;
}

// PHP 7.4 / 8.0 Backward Compatibility Polyfills
if (!function_exists('str_starts_with')) {
    function str_starts_with(?string $haystack, ?string $needle): bool {
        if ($haystack === null || $needle === null) {
            return false;
        }
        return '' === $needle || 0 === strncmp($haystack, $needle, strlen($needle));
    }
}
if (!function_exists('str_ends_with')) {
    function str_ends_with(?string $haystack, ?string $needle): bool {
        if ($haystack === null || $needle === null) {
            return false;
        }
        return '' === $needle || ('' !== $haystack && substr($haystack, -strlen($needle)) === $needle);
    }
}
if (!function_exists('str_contains')) {
    function str_contains(?string $haystack, ?string $needle): bool {
        if ($haystack === null || $needle === null) {
            return false;
        }
        return '' === $needle || false !== strpos($haystack, $needle);
    }
}

// Plugin constants
define('OMNI_AI_VERSION', '1.0.0');
define('OMNI_AI_FILE', __FILE__);
define('OMNI_AI_PATH', plugin_dir_path(__FILE__));
define('OMNI_AI_URL', plugin_dir_url(__FILE__));
define('OMNI_AI_LOGS_TABLE', 'ai_generation_logs');

// Load PSR-4 Autoloader
require_once OMNI_AI_PATH . 'includes/class-autoloader.php';

// Register activation and deactivation hooks
register_activation_hook(__FILE__, ['OmniAI\\Activator', 'activate']);
register_deactivation_hook(__FILE__, ['OmniAI\\Deactivator', 'deactivate']);

// Boot the plugin
add_action('plugins_loaded', function () {
    \OmniAI\Plugin::instance()->boot();
});

# Omni AI Suite - All-in-One AI Plugin for WordPress

A modular, high-performance "All-in-One" WordPress AI plugin combining the best capabilities of AI Engine, AI Power, and Bertha AI, while strictly avoiding their drawbacks (database bloat, messy UI, asset bloat, and server timeouts during bulk generation).

---

## 🌟 Key Features & Blueprint Implementations

### 1. Admin UI Architecture & Styling Strategy
- **Native React SPA:** Admin dashboard built as a Single Page Application exclusively utilizing `@wordpress/element` and `@wordpress/components`.
- **Zero Discrepancy & Maximum Performance:** Leverages WordPress core pre-loaded scripts and styles (`wp-components`, `wp-element`, `wp-i18n`, `wp-api-fetch`).
- **Scoped Tailwind CSS:**
  - `corePlugins: { preflight: false }` explicitly disabled in `tailwind.config.js`.
  - Prefix `tw-ai-` and strictly scoped inside `.omni-ai-admin-wrap`.
  - Zero CSS bleeding into WP core admin or other plugins.

### 2. Bring Your Own Key (BYOK)
- Connect your own API keys directly:
  - **Google Gemini** (Gemini 1.5 Flash, Gemini 1.5 Pro, Gemini 2.0 Flash)
  - **OpenAI** (GPT-4o, GPT-4o-mini, GPT-4 Turbo)
  - **Anthropic Claude** (Claude 3.5 Sonnet, Claude 3.5 Haiku, Claude 3 Opus)
- Safe encrypted storage; API keys are **never** exposed to frontend JavaScript or client-side scripts.
- Fallback integration with WordPress core `wp-admin/options-connectors.php` (WP AI Client Registry).

### 3. Real-Time Streaming Response System (SSE)
- **Backend:** Custom high-performance cURL streaming implementation using `CURLOPT_WRITEFUNCTION`, non-blocking buffers, and forced flushes (`ob_flush()`, `flush()`).
- **Frontend:** Standard `fetch()` with POST passing `X-WP-Nonce`, consuming `response.body.getReader()` with `TextDecoder("utf-8")` to decode chunks in real time with an animated cursor.

### 4. Zero Database Bloat (Custom Table Architecture)
- Does **NOT** use Custom Post Types (CPT) or `wp_postmeta` for generation logs.
- Automatically creates `{$wpdb->prefix}ai_generation_logs` via `dbDelta` upon activation.
- Post-stream saving: triggers a lightweight secondary REST call (`/save-log`) only once the stream reader finishes (`done === true`).

### 5. 100% Modular System
Every integration is an independent module with a toggle in **Modules Manager**:
- **AI Content Studio:** Real-time generation workbench with templates and post-to-draft.
- **Gutenberg Editor:** Injects sidebar actions via `wp.plugins.registerPlugin` to rewrite, polish, expand, or summarize selected blocks.
- **Elementor Editor:** Floating AI panel loaded exclusively inside Elementor via `elementor/editor/before_enqueue_scripts`.
- **WooCommerce Product Writer:** "AI Generate Description" button in general product data tabs.
- **Frontend Chatbot:** Embedded via shortcode `[omni_ai_chatbot]`. Strictly satisfies the anti-bloat rule: **zero** frontend scripts are enqueued unless the shortcode is actually present!
- **Asynchronous Bulk Generator:** Dispatches bulk post creation into WooCommerce Action Scheduler (`as_enqueue_async_action()`), preventing HTTP 504 Gateway Timeouts.

---

---

## 🛠️ Installation & Usage

1. Copy the `omni-ai-suite` directory into your WordPress `wp-content/plugins/` folder.
2. In the WordPress Admin, go to **Plugins** and activate **Omni AI Suite**.
3. Open **Omni AI** from the sidebar.
4. Go to **Settings (BYOK)** to enter your Google Gemini, OpenAI, or Claude API key.
5. Head to **Content Studio** to start streaming AI-generated content or use **Bulk Generator** for batch post creation!

=== Omni AI Suite ===
Contributors: (نام کاربری شما در سایت وردپرس)
Tags: ai, openai, gemini, claude, content generator, chatgpt
Requires at least: 6.0
Tested up to: 6.7
Stable tag: 1.0.0
License: GPLv2 or later
License URI: https://www.gnu.org/licenses/gpl-2.0.html

Omni AI Suite is a modular, high-performance AI Content Studio for WordPress with real-time SSE streaming, BYOK, and zero database bloat.

== Description ==
(توضیحات کامل افزونه را اینجا بنویسید. به قابلیت‌های کلیدی مثل تبدیل خودکار Mermaid به تصویر محلی SVG، پشتیبانی کامل از جداول و هدینگ‌های گوتنبرگ، و استریم زنده اشاره کنید.)

== Installation ==
1. Upload the plugin files to the `/wp-content/plugins/omni-ai-suite` directory, or install the plugin through the WordPress plugins screen directly.
2. Activate the plugin through the 'Plugins' screen in WordPress.
3. Go to the Omni AI Suite Settings and add your API keys (Gemini, OpenAI, or Claude).

== Screenshots ==
1. The AI Content Studio interface with real-time streaming.
2. Native Gutenberg output including Markdown tables and Sideloaded SVG diagrams.
3. Provider and Model dynamic selection settings.

== Changelog ==
= 1.0.0 =
* Initial release: Core Content Studio, dynamic models map, and Gutenberg native parser.

import { createElement, useState, useEffect } from '@wordpress/element';
import { __ } from '@wordpress/i18n';

import Header from './components/Header';
import ErrorBoundary from './components/ErrorBoundary';
import Dashboard from './pages/Dashboard';
import ContentStudio from './pages/ContentStudio';
import BulkGenerator from './pages/BulkGenerator';
import LogsHistory from './pages/LogsHistory';
import ModulesManager from './pages/ModulesManager';
import ApiSettings from './pages/ApiSettings';

export default function App() {
  const getInitialTab = () => {
    const hash = (window.location.hash || '').replace('#/', '').replace('#', '').trim();
    const validTabs = ['dashboard', 'studio', 'bulk', 'logs', 'modules', 'settings'];
    return validTabs.includes(hash) ? hash : 'dashboard';
  };

  const [activeTab, setActiveTab] = useState(getInitialTab);
  const [activeProvider, setActiveProvider] = useState('gemini');
  const [isConfigured, setIsConfigured] = useState(true);

  // Sync tab with browser URL hash
  useEffect(() => {
    const handleHashChange = () => {
      const hash = (window.location.hash || '').replace('#/', '').replace('#', '').trim();
      const validTabs = ['dashboard', 'studio', 'bulk', 'logs', 'modules', 'settings'];
      if (validTabs.includes(hash)) {
        setActiveTab(hash);
      }
    };

    window.addEventListener('hashchange', handleHashChange);
    return () => window.removeEventListener('hashchange', handleHashChange);
  }, []);

  const handleSelectTab = (tabName) => {
    setActiveTab(tabName);
    window.location.hash = `#/${tabName}`;
  };

  const tabs = [
    {
      name: 'dashboard',
      title: __('Dashboard', 'omni-ai-suite'),
      className: 'omni-ai-tab-dashboard',
    },
    {
      name: 'studio',
      title: __('AI Content Studio', 'omni-ai-suite'),
      className: 'omni-ai-tab-studio',
    },
    {
      name: 'bulk',
      title: __('Bulk Generator', 'omni-ai-suite'),
      className: 'omni-ai-tab-bulk',
    },
    {
      name: 'logs',
      title: __('History & Logs', 'omni-ai-suite'),
      className: 'omni-ai-tab-logs',
    },
    {
      name: 'modules',
      title: __('Modules', 'omni-ai-suite'),
      className: 'omni-ai-tab-modules',
    },
    {
      name: 'settings',
      title: __('Settings (BYOK)', 'omni-ai-suite'),
      className: 'omni-ai-tab-settings',
    },
  ];

  const renderActiveView = () => {
    switch (activeTab) {
      case 'dashboard':
        return <Dashboard onNavigate={handleSelectTab} />;
      case 'studio':
        return <ContentStudio />;
      case 'bulk':
        return <BulkGenerator />;
      case 'logs':
        return <LogsHistory onNavigate={handleSelectTab} />;
      case 'modules':
        return <ModulesManager />;
      case 'settings':
        return (
          <ApiSettings
            onSettingsUpdated={(newSettings) => {
              if (newSettings?.default_provider) {
                setActiveProvider(newSettings.default_provider);
              }
            }}
          />
        );
      default:
        return <Dashboard onNavigate={handleSelectTab} />;
    }
  };

  return (
    <div>
      <Header configured={isConfigured} activeProvider={activeProvider} />

      <div className="omni-ai-nav-panel">
        <div className="components-tab-panel__tabs" role="tablist" style={{ display: 'flex', gap: '4px' }}>
          {tabs.map((tab) => {
            const isActive = activeTab === tab.name;
            return (
              <button
                key={tab.name}
                type="button"
                role="tab"
                aria-selected={isActive}
                className={`components-button components-tab-panel__tabs-item ${tab.className || ''} ${isActive ? 'is-active active' : ''}`}
                onClick={() => handleSelectTab(tab.name)}
                style={{
                  cursor: 'pointer',
                  borderBottom: isActive ? '3px solid #2271b1' : '3px solid transparent',
                  fontWeight: isActive ? 600 : 400,
                  color: isActive ? '#1d2327' : '#50575e',
                  padding: '12px 18px',
                  background: 'none',
                  borderTop: 'none',
                  borderLeft: 'none',
                  borderRight: 'none',
                  fontSize: '14px',
                  outline: 'none',
                }}
              >
                {tab.title}
              </button>
            );
          })}
        </div>

        <div className="components-tab-panel__tab-content" role="tabpanel">
          <ErrorBoundary>
            {renderActiveView()}
          </ErrorBoundary>
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

import { Component, createElement } from '@wordpress/element';
import { Button } from '@wordpress/components';
import { __ } from '@wordpress/i18n';

export default class ErrorBoundary extends Component {
  constructor(props) {
    super(props);
    this.state = { hasError: false, error: null };
  }

  static getDerivedStateFromError(error) {
    return { hasError: true, error };
  }

  componentDidCatch(error, errorInfo) {
    console.error('[Omni AI Suite] Page Rendering Crash Caught:', error, errorInfo);
  }

  render() {
    if (this.state.hasError) {
      return (
        <div style={{ padding: '30px', textAlign: 'center', background: '#fff', borderRadius: '4px', border: '1px solid #c3c4c7', margin: '20px 0' }}>
          <h3 style={{ color: '#d63638', marginBottom: '10px' }}>
            {__('⚠️ An error occurred while rendering this view.', 'omni-ai-suite')}
          </h3>
          <p style={{ color: '#646970', fontSize: '13px', maxWidth: '600px', margin: '0 auto 16px' }}>
            {this.state.error?.message || __('Unknown view rendering error.', 'omni-ai-suite')}
          </p>
          <Button
            variant="secondary"
            onClick={() => {
              this.setState({ hasError: false, error: null });
              if (window.location.hash) {
                window.location.reload();
              }
            }}
          >
            {__('Try Again / Reload', 'omni-ai-suite')}
          </Button>
        </div>
      );
    }
    return this.props.children;
  }
}

import { createElement } from '@wordpress/element';
import { Notice } from '@wordpress/components';

export default function NoticeBar({ notice, onDismiss }) {
  if (!notice) return null;

  return (
    <div style={{ marginBottom: '16px' }}>
      <Notice
        status={notice.type || 'info'}
        isDismissible={true}
        onDismiss={onDismiss}
      >
        {notice.message}
      </Notice>
    </div>
  );
}

### FILE: src\admin\hooks\useAiStream.js
### SIZE: 6895 bytes
### PATH: I:\NextJS Projects\zebracode\zebracode\wordpress\plugins\omni-ai-suite\src\admin\hooks\useAiStream.js
################################################################################

import { useState, useCallback, useRef } from '@wordpress/element';
import { buildApiUrl, apiFetch } from '../utils/api';

/**
 * Custom React hook for real-time Server-Sent Events (SSE) AI streaming.
 * Adheres to Phase 2 & Phase 3 specifications:
 * - Uses POST fetch with X-WP-Nonce
 * - Reads chunks with response.body.getReader() & TextDecoder
 * - Incremental DOM updates
 * - Automatically triggers secondary REST API call upon completion to save to custom table
 */
export function useAiStream() {
  const [streaming, setStreaming] = useState(false);
  const [output, setOutput] = useState('');
  const [error, setError] = useState(null);
  const abortControllerRef = useRef(null);

  const startStream = useCallback(async ({
    prompt,
    provider,
    model,
    systemPrompt = '',
    temperature = 0.7,
    maxTokens = 2048,
    onFinish = null,
  }) => {
    if (!prompt || !prompt.trim()) {
      setError('Prompt is required.');
      return;
    }

    setStreaming(true);
    setOutput('');
    setError(null);

    const abortController = new AbortController();
    abortControllerRef.current = abortController;

    const nonce = window.omniAiData?.nonce || '';
    let accumulatedText = '';

    try {
      const streamBody = JSON.stringify({
        prompt,
        provider,
        model,
        system_prompt: systemPrompt,
        temperature,
        max_tokens: maxTokens,
      });

      let streamUrl = buildApiUrl('stream-ai');
      let response = await fetch(streamUrl, {
        method: 'POST',
        credentials: 'same-origin',
        headers: {
          'Content-Type': 'application/json',
          'X-WP-Nonce': nonce,
        },
        body: streamBody,
        signal: abortController.signal,
      });

      // Retry with plain route if 404
      if (response.status === 404 && !streamUrl.includes('rest_route=')) {
        streamUrl = buildApiUrl('stream-ai', {}, true);
        response = await fetch(streamUrl, {
          method: 'POST',
          credentials: 'same-origin',
          headers: {
            'Content-Type': 'application/json',
            'X-WP-Nonce': nonce,
          },
          body: streamBody,
          signal: abortController.signal,
        });
      }

      if (!response.ok) {
        let errorMessage = `HTTP error ${response.status}: ${response.statusText}`;
        try {
          const errData = await response.json();
          if (errData && (errData.message || errData.error)) {
            errorMessage = errData.message || errData.error;
          }
        } catch (_) {
          // If response is not JSON or stream text, fallback to status text
        }
        setError(errorMessage);
        setStreaming(false);
        abortControllerRef.current = null;

        // Log connection/HTTP failure
        try {
          await apiFetch('save-log', {
            method: 'POST',
            headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
            body: JSON.stringify({
              prompt,
              response: `ERROR: ${errorMessage}`,
              model: model || 'default',
              provider: provider || 'default',
              tokens: 0,
            }),
          });
        } catch (_) {}

        return;
      }

      if (!response.body) {
        throw new Error('ReadableStream not supported by this browser.');
      }

      const reader = response.body.getReader();
      const decoder = new TextDecoder('utf-8');
      let buffer = '';
      let hasError = false;
      let streamErrorMessage = '';

      while (true) {
        const { value, done } = await reader.read();
        if (done) {
          break;
        }

        buffer += decoder.decode(value, { stream: true });
        const lines = buffer.split('\n');
        buffer = lines.pop() || ''; // Keep unfinished line in buffer

        for (const line of lines) {
          const trimmed = line.trim();
          if (trimmed.startsWith('data:')) {
            const dataStr = trimmed.slice(5).trim();
            if (!dataStr) continue;

            let parsed = null;
            try {
              parsed = JSON.parse(dataStr);
            } catch (err) {
              continue;
            }

            if (parsed.error) {
              streamErrorMessage = parsed.error;
              setError(parsed.error);
              hasError = true;
              break;
            }
            if (parsed.text) {
              accumulatedText += parsed.text;
              setOutput((prev) => prev + parsed.text);
            }
            if (parsed.done) {
              break;
            }
          }
        }
        if (hasError) break;
      }

      // Phase 3: Post-stream saving to custom table (zero CPT/meta bloat)
      if (hasError) {
        try {
          await apiFetch('save-log', {
            method: 'POST',
            headers: {
              'Content-Type': 'application/json',
            },
            body: JSON.stringify({
              prompt,
              response: `ERROR: ${streamErrorMessage || 'Unknown streaming error'}`,
              model: model || 'default',
              provider: provider || 'default',
              tokens: 0,
            }),
          });
        } catch (_) {}
      } else if (accumulatedText.trim().length > 0) {
        try {
          await apiFetch('save-log', {
            method: 'POST',
            headers: {
              'Content-Type': 'application/json',
            },
            body: JSON.stringify({
              prompt,
              response: accumulatedText,
              model: model || 'default',
              provider: provider || 'default',
              tokens: Math.round(accumulatedText.split(/\s+/).length * 1.3),
            }),
          });
        } catch (saveErr) {
          console.warn('[Omni AI Suite] Log save failed:', saveErr);
        }
      }

      if (typeof onFinish === 'function') {
        onFinish(accumulatedText);
      }
    } catch (err) {
      if (err.name !== 'AbortError') {
        const errMsg = err.message || 'Stream connection error occurred.';
        setError(errMsg);
        try {
          await apiFetch('save-log', {
            method: 'POST',
            headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
            body: JSON.stringify({
              prompt,
              response: `ERROR: ${errMsg}`,
              model: model || 'default',
              provider: provider || 'default',
              tokens: 0,
            }),
          });
        } catch (_) {}
      }
    } finally {
      setStreaming(false);
      abortControllerRef.current = null;
    }
  }, []);

  const stopStream = useCallback(() => {
    if (abortControllerRef.current) {
      abortControllerRef.current.abort();
      setStreaming(false);
    }
  }, []);

  return {
    streaming,
    output,
    setOutput,
    error,
    startStream,
    stopStream,
  };
}

import { createElement, render, createRoot } from '@wordpress/element';
import App from './App';

// Global sprintf polyfill to prevent any 3rd party or WordPress i18n reference crashes
if (typeof window !== 'undefined') {
  if (typeof window.sprintf !== 'function') {
    window.sprintf = (format, ...args) => {
      if (typeof window.wp?.i18n?.sprintf === 'function') {
        return window.wp.i18n.sprintf(format, ...args);
      }
      let i = 0;
      return String(format || '').replace(/%[sdf1-9\$]*/g, () => args[i++] ?? '');
    };
  }
}

document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', () => {
  const container = document.getElementById('omni-ai-admin-root');
  if (!container) return;

  if (createRoot) {
    const root = createRoot(container);
    root.render(createElement(App));
  } else if (render) {
    render(createElement(App), container);
  }
});

    default_provider: 'gemini',
    default_image_provider: 'gemini',
    gemini_api_key: '',
    gemini_model: 'gemini-1.5-flash',
    openai_api_key: '',
    openai_model: 'gpt-4o-mini',
    claude_api_key: '',
    claude_model: 'claude-3-5-sonnet-20241022',
    temperature: 0.7,
    max_tokens: 2000,
  });

  const [maskedState, setMaskedState] = useState({
    gemini_api_key_masked: '',
    gemini_api_key_configured: false,
    openai_api_key_masked: '',
    openai_api_key_configured: false,
    claude_api_key_masked: '',
    claude_api_key_configured: false,
  });

  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);
  const [saving, setSaving] = useState(false);
  const [notice, setNotice] = useState(null);

  useEffect(() => {
    fetchSettings();
  }, []);

  const fetchSettings = async () => {
    try {
      const res = await apiFetch('settings');
      const data = await res.json();
      if (data.settings) {
        setSettings({
          default_provider: data.settings.default_provider || 'gemini',
          default_image_provider: data.settings.default_image_provider || 'gemini',
          gemini_api_key: '',
          gemini_model: data.settings.gemini_model || 'gemini-1.5-flash',
          openai_api_key: '',
          openai_model: data.settings.openai_model || 'gpt-4o-mini',
          claude_api_key: '',
          claude_model: data.settings.claude_model || 'claude-3-5-sonnet-20241022',
          temperature: parseFloat(data.settings.temperature) || 0.7,
          max_tokens: parseInt(data.settings.max_tokens, 10) || 2000,
        });

        setMaskedState({
          gemini_api_key_masked: data.settings.gemini_api_key_masked || '',
          gemini_api_key_configured: !!data.settings.gemini_api_key_configured,
          openai_api_key_masked: data.settings.openai_api_key_masked || '',
          openai_api_key_configured: !!data.settings.openai_api_key_configured,
          claude_api_key_masked: data.settings.claude_api_key_masked || '',
          claude_api_key_configured: !!data.settings.claude_api_key_configured,
        });
      }
    } catch (err) {
      setNotice({ type: 'error', message: 'Failed to load settings.' });
    } finally {
      setLoading(false);
    }
  };

  const handleSave = async (e) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    setSaving(true);
    setNotice(null);

    try {
      const payload = {
        default_provider: settings.default_provider,
        default_image_provider: settings.default_image_provider,
        gemini_model: settings.gemini_model,
        openai_model: settings.openai_model,
        claude_model: settings.claude_model,
        temperature: settings.temperature,
        max_tokens: settings.max_tokens,
      };

      if (settings.gemini_api_key.trim()) {
        payload.gemini_api_key = settings.gemini_api_key.trim();
      }
      if (settings.openai_api_key.trim()) {
        payload.openai_api_key = settings.openai_api_key.trim();
      }
      if (settings.claude_api_key.trim()) {
        payload.claude_api_key = settings.claude_api_key.trim();
      }

      const res = await apiFetch('settings', {
        method: 'POST',
        headers: {
          'Content-Type': 'application/json',
        },
        body: JSON.stringify(payload),
      });

      const data = await res.json();
      if (res.ok && data.success) {
        setNotice({ type: 'success', message: __('Settings saved successfully.', 'omni-ai-suite') });
        fetchSettings();
        if (typeof onSettingsUpdated === 'function') {
          onSettingsUpdated(payload);
        }
      } else {
        throw new Error(data.message || 'Failed to save settings.');
      }
    } catch (err) {
      setNotice({ type: 'error', message: err.message });
    } finally {
      setSaving(false);
    }
  };

    return (

        <Spinner />

    );

              />

              <SelectControl
                label={__('Default Image Generation Engine', 'omni-ai-suite')}
                value={settings.default_image_provider || 'gemini'}
                options={[
                  { label: 'Google Gemini (Flash Image / Nano Banana)', value: 'gemini' },

import { createElement, useState } from '@wordpress/element';
import {
  Card,
  CardBody,
  CardHeader,
  Button,
  TextareaControl,
  SelectControl,
  Notice,
  Spinner,
} from '@wordpress/components';
import { __ } from '@wordpress/i18n';
import { apiFetch } from '../utils/api';

export default function BulkGenerator() {
  const [promptsInput, setPromptsInput] = useState('');
  const [provider, setProvider] = useState('gemini');
  const [model, setModel] = useState('gemini-1.5-flash');
  const [postStatus, setPostStatus] = useState('draft');
  const [postType, setPostType] = useState('post');
  const [systemPrompt, setSystemPrompt] = useState(
    'Write a comprehensive, SEO-optimized article with headings (H2, H3), bullet points, and actionable tips.'
  );
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(false);
  const [feedback, setFeedback] = useState(null);

  const handleSubmit = async (e) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    const rawLines = promptsInput
      .split('\n')
      .map((line) => line.trim())
      .filter((line) => line.length > 0);

    if (rawLines.length === 0) {
      setFeedback({ type: 'error', message: __('Please enter at least one topic or prompt.', 'omni-ai-suite') });
      return;
    }

    setLoading(true);
    setFeedback(null);

    try {
      const promptsList = rawLines.map((line) => ({
        title: line,
        prompt: `Write a complete blog post on the topic: ${line}`,
      }));

      const res = await apiFetch('bulk', {
        method: 'POST',
        headers: {
          'Content-Type': 'application/json',
        },
        body: JSON.stringify({
          prompts: promptsList,
          provider,
          model,
          system_prompt: systemPrompt,
          post_status: postStatus,
          post_type: postType,
        }),
      });

      const data = await res.json();
      if (res.ok && data.success) {
        setFeedback({
          type: 'success',
          message: data.message || `${rawLines.length} tasks successfully enqueued into background worker!`,
        });
        setPromptsInput('');
      } else {
        throw new Error(data.message || 'Failed to enqueue tasks.');
      }
    } catch (err) {
      setFeedback({ type: 'error', message: err.message || 'Error occurred while scheduling background tasks.' });
    } finally {
      setLoading(false);
    }
  };

  const lineCount = promptsInput
    .split('\n')
    .filter((l) => l.trim().length > 0).length;

  return (
    <div style={{ maxWidth: '800px' }}>
      <Card>
        <CardHeader>
          <div>
            <strong>{__('⚡ Asynchronous Bulk Content Generation', 'omni-ai-suite')}</strong>
            <p style={{ margin: '4px 0 0', fontSize: '13px', color: '#646970' }}>
              {__(
                'Powered by Action Scheduler: jobs are dispatched asynchronously in background workers, completely eliminating HTTP 504 timeouts.',
                'omni-ai-suite'
              )}
            </p>
          </div>
        </CardHeader>
        <CardBody>
          {feedback && (
            <div style={{ marginBottom: '16px' }}>
              <Notice status={feedback.type} onDismiss={() => setFeedback(null)}>
                {feedback.message}
              </Notice>
            </div>
          )}

          <form onSubmit={handleSubmit}>
            <div style={{ marginBottom: '16px' }}>
              <TextareaControl
                label={__('Article Titles or Topics (One per line)', 'omni-ai-suite')}
                value={promptsInput}
                onChange={setPromptsInput}
                rows={8}
                placeholder={__(
                  "Top 10 Web Development Trends in 2026\nHow to Optimize Next.js Performance\nBeginner Guide to Tailwind CSS\nCybersecurity Best Practices for WordPress",
                  'omni-ai-suite'
                )}
                help={
                  lineCount > 0
                    ? `${lineCount} ${__('articles detected to generate.', 'omni-ai-suite')}`
                    : __('Enter one topic per line. Each will become a dedicated draft post.', 'omni-ai-suite')
                }
              />
            </div>

            <div style={{ marginBottom: '16px' }}>
              <TextareaControl
                label={__('System Instructions Template', 'omni-ai-suite')}
                value={systemPrompt}
                onChange={setSystemPrompt}
                rows={2}
              />
            </div>

            <div style={{ display: 'grid', gridTemplateColumns: 'repeat(auto-fit, minmax(180px, 1fr))', gap: '16px', marginBottom: '20px' }}>
              <SelectControl
                label={__('AI Provider', 'omni-ai-suite')}
                value={provider}
                options={[
                  { label: 'Google Gemini', value: 'gemini' },
                  { label: 'OpenAI', value: 'openai' },
                  { label: 'Anthropic Claude', value: 'claude' },
                ]}
                onChange={setProvider}
              />

              <SelectControl
                label={__('Model', 'omni-ai-suite')}
                value={model}
                options={
                  provider === 'gemini'
                    ? [
                        { label: 'Gemini 1.5 Flash', value: 'gemini-1.5-flash' },
                        { label: 'Gemini 1.5 Pro', value: 'gemini-1.5-pro' },
                      ]
                    : provider === 'openai'
                    ? [
                        { label: 'GPT-4o Mini', value: 'gpt-4o-mini' },
                        { label: 'GPT-4o', value: 'gpt-4o' },
                      ]
                    : [
                        { label: 'Claude 3.5 Haiku', value: 'claude-3-5-haiku-20241022' },
                        { label: 'Claude 3.5 Sonnet', value: 'claude-3-5-sonnet-20241022' },
                      ]
                }
                onChange={setModel}
              />

              <SelectControl
                label={__('Created Post Status', 'omni-ai-suite')}
                value={postStatus}
                options={[
                  { label: __('Draft (Recommended)', 'omni-ai-suite'), value: 'draft' },
                  { label: __('Pending Review', 'omni-ai-suite'), value: 'pending' },
                  { label: __('Private', 'omni-ai-suite'), value: 'private' },
                ]}
                onChange={setPostStatus}
              />

              <SelectControl
                label={__('Post Type', 'omni-ai-suite')}
                value={postType}
                options={[
                  { label: __('Posts (Blog)', 'omni-ai-suite'), value: 'post' },
                  { label: __('Pages', 'omni-ai-suite'), value: 'page' },
                ]}
                onChange={setPostType}
              />
            </div>

            <div style={{ display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', gap: '12px' }}>
              <Button
                variant="primary"
                type="submit"
                disabled={loading || lineCount === 0}
              >
                {loading ? (
                  <>
                    <Spinner /> {__('Queueing Jobs...', 'omni-ai-suite')}
                  </>
                ) : (
                  `${__('🚀 Enqueue Articles to Background Queue', 'omni-ai-suite')} (${Math.max(1, lineCount)})`
                )}
              </Button>
            </div>
          </form>
        </CardBody>
      </Card>
    </div>
  );
}
